# Chapter One Part A excerpt — access and opportunity

## English source passage

This subsection sets out what access and opportunity mean in practice:

- Sentients need practical paths to [Participation](core_05.025_apex_participation_leg.md#participation-constitutional), education, work, care, safety, movement, and other goods that matter to ordinary life.
- Those paths must not be blocked, priced out, delayed, hidden, or tilted for arbitrary or irrelevant reasons.
- A door that is open only on paper is not enough where this Constitution requires **substantive** opportunity.

## Draft translation (zh)

本小节说明实践中的可及性与机会意味着什么：

- 有感知能力的存在需要切实可行的途径，来获得[参与](core_05.025_apex_participation_leg.md#participation-constitutional)、教育、工作、照护、安全、行动自由以及其他日常生活所需的重要事物。
- 不得以任意或无关理由阻断这些途径、将价格抬高到无法承受、拖延、隐瞒或加以偏置。
- 当本宪法要求**实质性**机会时，仅在纸面上敞开的门并不足够。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §7.1 — Meaningful Agency and Consent

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.1 Limitation Discipline

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

[Meaningful Agency](core_05.055_band_participation.md#meaningful-agency) is the ability to make a choice between real options. [Consent](core_05.055_band_participation.md#consent) is a valid yes to one specific option. Consent does not count when the options are not distinct from each other or are not meaningful. A signed form alone creates neither meaningful agency nor consent. [Coercion and Manipulation](core_05.055_band_participation.md#coercion-and-manipulation) destroy both. The detailed rules are in Chapter Five §2 *Agency, consent, and anti-coercion*.

## Draft translation (zh)

[有意义的能动性](core_05.055_band_participation.md#meaningful-agency)是指能够在真实选项之间作出选择。[同意](core_05.055_band_participation.md#consent)是对某一具体选项作出的有效肯定。如果选项彼此没有区别或没有实际意义，同意就不成立。仅有签名表格既不能产生有意义的能动性，也不能构成同意。[胁迫与操纵](core_05.055_band_participation.md#coercion-and-manipulation)会摧毁二者。详细规则见第五章§2“能动性、同意与反胁迫”。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One excerpt — how the aims are developed

## English source

**Where Chapter One develops each aim.** Flourishing is wellbeing sustained through truth, safety, trustworthiness, and meaningful agency ([Preamble §1](core_00.000_preamble.md#flourishing)). Chapter One states the outcome first, then one principle for each of the four conditions.

## zh draft

**第一章如何展开各项目标。** Flourishing 是通过真理、安全、可信赖性和有意义的能动性维持的福祉（[序言§1](core_00.000_preamble.md#flourishing)）。第一章先陈述结果，再为四项条件各提出一条原则。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__aims_tetrad_two_axes.md bytes=1538 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One Part A reader guidance, relationship between the Aims and Tetrad; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One excerpt — the Aims and Tetrad as two axes

## English source

**Two axes, not a conflict.** The aims say what systems pursue; the Tetrad says how that pursuit stays legitimate. A term can sit on both: Truth and Trustworthiness are constituents of Flourishing, and [Preamble §2](core_00.000_preamble.md#2-measurements-overview) measures them under the Oversight family. [§§13–15](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#13-constitutional-collision-resolution-process) govern how these principles are weighed when they collide and read as one whole, and [§20](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#20-integrated-application) applies them to every later chapter.

## zh draft

**两条轴线，并非冲突。**两个目标说明系统追求什么；四元结构说明这种追求如何保持正当性。一个术语可以同时属于两条轴线：Truth 和 Trustworthiness 是 Flourishing 的组成部分，[序言§2](core_00.000_preamble.md#2-measurements-overview)将其纳入 Oversight 家族进行衡量。[§§13–15](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#13-constitutional-collision-resolution-process)规定这些原则冲突时如何权衡并作为整体解读；[§20](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#20-integrated-application)将其应用于之后的每一章。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__aligned_recognition_pathways.md bytes=1395 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One Part A §3.2.5 Aligned Recognition Pathways; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — aligned recognition pathways

## English source passage

Named recognition pathways, acclaim, prizes, certification, standing, reputation effects, or comparable incentives that **allocate status**, **resources**, or **material access** must remain consistent with Truth, Safety, contestable procedure where **Chapter Six** assigns it, [Participation](core_05.025_apex_participation_leg.md#participation-constitutional), and [Incentive Alignment](core_05.045_band_integrative.md#incentive-alignment).

They **must not** systematically reward harm, deception, avoidance of scrutiny, extraction, or erosion of meaningful agency.

## Draft translation (zh)

任何分配**地位**、**资源**或**实质性访问权**的指定认可渠道、赞誉、奖项、认证、身份地位、声誉影响或类似激励，都必须符合真实、安全，以及**第六章**规定的可争议程序，并与[参与](core_05.025_apex_participation_leg.md#participation-constitutional)和[激励协调](core_05.045_band_integrative.md#incentive-alignment)保持一致。

这些机制**不得**系统性地奖励伤害、欺骗、逃避审查、攫取或削弱有意义的自主性。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__aligned_self_org_no_approval.md bytes=999 -->
# Chapter One §7.2.1 — Receipt Is Not Approval

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.2.1 Aligned Self-Organization

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Being received, routed, or answered is not approval of the authors' conclusions. Any governing or merits effect requires the separate lawful authority, evidence, fair process, review, and remedy this Constitution assigns. The full no-self-appointment rule lives in [§17.4 Aligned Self-Organization](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#174-aligned-self-organization).

## Draft translation (zh)

接收、转送或答复该工作，并不表示认可作者的结论。任何治理或实体效力，都必须具备本宪章指定的独立合法权限、证据、公平程序、审查和救济。禁止自行任命的完整规则见[§17.4 协调一致的自我组织](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#174-aligned-self-organization)。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__aligned_self_org_no_power.md bytes=1262 -->
# Chapter One §7.2.1 — Procedural Path Does Not Grant Power

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.2.1 Aligned Self-Organization

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

That path is not a grant of power. Starting, running, funding, publishing, or submitting the work does not, by itself:
- give anyone the power to govern others, enforce against them, or coerce them
- bind sentients who did not agree to a substantive outcome
- decide standing, liability, entitlement, validity, a mandate, a remedy, a classification, or a rights restriction
- count as a [Merits Determination](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#merits-determination) — a binding decision on the substance of the dispute

## Draft translation (zh)

该程序路径并不授予权力。启动、开展、资助、发布或提交该工作本身并不会：
- 赋予任何人治理他人、对其实施强制措施或胁迫他人的权力
- 约束未同意实质性结果的有感知生命
- 决定诉讼资格、责任、权利、有效性、授权、救济、分类或权利限制
- 构成[实体裁决](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#merits-determination)，即对争议实质作出的有约束力决定

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__aligned_self_org_opening.md bytes=1488 -->
# Chapter One §7.2.1 — Aligned Self-Organization Opening

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.2.1 Aligned Self-Organization

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: you may start legitimate work without a sponsor, and institutions must give credible work a real procedural path — but that path is not power to govern others, and it is not a final decision on the substance.*

The freedom to assemble or create a system includes a real way to start work this Constitution treats as legitimate, without waiting for stewards already in charge to sponsor it. [§17.4 Aligned Self-Organization](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#174-aligned-self-organization) is the operative home. This subsection applies that rule at the Freedom / assembly-and-formation layer.

## Draft translation (zh)

*简而言之：你可以在没有赞助者的情况下启动正当工作，机构必须为可信的工作提供真实的程序路径；但该路径不赋予治理他人的权力，也不是对实质问题的最终裁决。*

集会或创建系统的自由包括一种真实方式，使人们无需等待现任受托人的赞助即可启动本宪章视为正当的工作。[§17.4 协调一致的自我组织](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#174-aligned-self-organization)是该规则的实际依据。本小节在自由、集会与形成这一层面适用该规则。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__aligned_self_org_procedure.md bytes=792 -->
# Chapter One §7.2.1 — Procedural Path for Credible Work

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.2.1 Aligned Self-Organization

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

When that work is credible and materially relevant, institutions must give it a real procedural path:
- receive it
- preserve it where warranted
- route it
- give a reasoned response
- have it reviewed by someone independent of those whose actions are being examined

## Draft translation (zh)

当该工作可信且具有实质相关性时，机构必须为其提供真实的程序路径：
- 接收
- 在有需要时予以保存
- 转送至适当渠道
- 给出有理由的答复
- 由独立于被审查行为相关人员的人进行审查

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# §3.3 Anti-Degrading Process — Design and Conduct

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §3.3

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Design and conduct.** Processes must not be designed, framed, carried out, or allowed to operate as degradation, humiliation, spectacle, retaliation, discriminatory burdening, or convenience-driven rights erosion.

## Draft translation (zh)

**设计与实施。** 不得将程序设计、包装、执行或放任其运行成贬损、羞辱、示众、报复、施加歧视性负担，或为图方便而侵蚀权利的做法。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__anti_degrading_plain_language.md bytes=1358 -->
# §3.3 Anti-Degrading Process — Plain-Language Explanation

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §3.3

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: however you govern, enforce, judge, restrict, or remedy — you don't run sentients through humiliation, public spectacle, retaliation, or "because it's easier for us" cruelty. Fair consequences, public accountability, and firm restrictions can still be lawful even when they hurt or embarrass someone. What crosses the line is when the process itself is the punishment — designed to degrade, shame, or lash out rather than to protect, correct, restore, or prevent. This applies everywhere constitutional authority runs, not only during rights tradeoffs.*

## Draft translation (zh)

*简单来说：无论治理、执法、裁判、限制还是补救，都不得以“这样对我们更方便”为由，让有感知能力的存在遭受羞辱、公开示众、报复或残酷对待。公平的后果、公开问责和坚定的限制，即使会使某人受伤或难堪，仍可能合法。越界之处在于程序本身变成惩罚——其设计目的在于贬损、羞辱或发泄，而非保护、纠正、恢复或预防。本原则适用于宪法权力行使的所有场合，不仅限于权利权衡时。*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__anti_degrading_prohibited_conditions.md bytes=1075 -->
# §3.3 Anti-Degrading Process — Prohibited Conditions

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §3.3

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Anti-Degrading-Process Principle.** Constitutional processes, measures, and outcomes must satisfy this principle.

**Prohibited.** They must not be justified by, include, or predictably create:

- degrading treatment;
- humiliation for its own sake;
- spectacle used mainly to deter;
- retaliatory grievance;
- collective retaliation;
- discriminatory burdening; or
- procedural convenience overriding rights.

## Draft translation (zh)

**非贬损程序原则。** 宪法程序、措施和结果都必须符合本原则。

**禁止事项。** 不得以以下事项作为正当理由，不得包含以下事项，也不得可预见地造成以下事项：

- 贬损人格的待遇；
- 为羞辱而羞辱；
- 主要用于威慑的示众；
- 报复性申诉；
- 集体报复；
- 施加歧视性负担；或
- 让程序便利凌驾于权利之上。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__anti_degrading_scope_challenge.md bytes=2218 -->
# §3.3 Anti-Degrading Process — Scope and Challenge

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §3.3

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Scope.** This principle applies to every constitutional process, including:

- governance and implementation decisions;
- enforcement and standing assessment;
- forum proceedings;
- emergency measures and Adoption Transition plans;
- amendment procedures; and
- all administrative and operational activities under constitutional authority.

It is not limited to the tradeoff-stack context in which it also operates as an absolute floor under [§13.1.4 Constitutional Floors, Safety, and Anti-Degrading Process](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#1314-constitutional-floors-safety-and-anti-degrading-process).

**Detection and challenge.** The character of a process is subject to the same [Contestability](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#contestability) and [Oversight](core_05.020_apex_oversight_leg.md#oversight-constitutional) requirements as substantive outcomes. Affected parties may challenge the character of a process independently of whether the substantive outcome would otherwise be lawful. A correct outcome delivered through degrading process remains non-compliant.

## Draft translation (zh)

**适用范围。** 本原则适用于每一项宪法程序，包括：

- 治理与实施决定；
- 执行与诉讼资格评估；
- 论坛程序；
- 紧急措施和采纳过渡计划；
- 修正程序；以及
- 宪法权限下的所有行政和运营活动。

本原则不限于权衡序列的情境；在该情境中，它也依照[§13.1.4 宪法底线、安全与不贬损程序](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#1314-constitutional-floors-safety-and-anti-degrading-process)发挥绝对底线作用。

**发现与挑战。** 程序的性质须满足与实体结果相同的[可争议性](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#contestability)和[监督](core_05.020_apex_oversight_leg.md#oversight-constitutional)要求。受影响方可以独立于实体结果原本是否合法，挑战程序的性质。通过贬损性程序得出的正确结果仍然不合规。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__anti_degrading_what_it_is.md bytes=1632 -->
# §3.3 Anti-Degrading Process — What It Is

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §3.3

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**What it is:** a cross-cutting character floor under [§3 Wellbeing](#3-foundational-objective-wellbeing-flourishing-aim) — it does not add substantive work of its own the way [§3.1 Fairness](#31-fairness) and [§3.2 Recognition, Reinforcement, and Aspiration](#32-recognition-reinforcement-and-aspiration) do, but constrains *how* wellbeing work, stewardship work, and every other constitutional process may be carried out — including the three pillars of [§16 Stewardship In Depth](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#16-stewardship-in-depth) and the steward role defined in [§17 Consequential Stewardship](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#17-consequential-stewardship-the-steward-role).

## Draft translation (zh)

**它是什么：**这是[§3 福祉](#3-foundational-objective-wellbeing-flourishing-aim)下的一项跨领域行为底线。它不像[§3.1 公平](#31-fairness)和[§3.2 认可、强化与追求](#32-recognition-reinforcement-and-aspiration)那样增添自身的实质性任务，而是约束福祉工作、受托治理工作以及其他所有宪法程序的*实施方式*，包括[§16 深入理解受托治理](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#16-stewardship-in-depth)的三大支柱，以及[§17 对后果负责的受托治理](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#17-consequential-stewardship-the-steward-role)所界定的受托人角色。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__anti_reward_plain_language.md bytes=1020 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One Part A §3.2.6 Anti-Reward plain-language rule; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — anti-reward for anti-constitutional conduct

## English source passage

*In plain terms: no one may be rewarded for committing, hiding, or retaliating over anti-constitutional conduct, whether openly or through back doors like favors, quiet promotions, or looking the other way. Helping the sentients who were harmed, and protecting those who report honestly, is different. That is the right response, and it should be encouraged.*

## Draft translation (zh)

*简而言之：任何人都不得因实施或掩盖违宪行为，或报复诚实举报者而获奖；无论是公开行为，还是通过偏袒、暗中晋升、故意视而不见等暗门手段。帮助受害的有感知能力的存在、保护诚实举报者则是另一回事。这才是正确的回应，应当予以鼓励。*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §11.2.2 — Durable-Power Prohibition

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §11.2.2 Anti-Domination Prohibitions (Don'ts)

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

None of the following:
- sentient(s);
- institution(s);
- platform(s);
- enterprise(s);
- state organ(s);
- steward(s);
- coordinated group(s)

may:
- create;
- maintain;
- acquire;
- exploit;
- conceal;
- restructure around

durable power of any of the following kinds:
- market;
- platform;
- infrastructure;
- labor;
- supplier;
- data;
- compute;
- credentialing;
- capital-access;
- resource-control.

where that power foreseeably degrades:
- wellbeing;
- meaningful agency;
- fair compensation;
- innovation;
- access;
- ecological integrity;
- contestability;
- constitutional review.


## Draft translation (zh)

以下任何主体均不得：
- 有感知者；
- 机构；
- 平台；
- 企业；
- 国家机关；
- 受托管理者；
- 协同行动的群体

实施以下行为：
- 创造；
- 维持；
- 获取；
- 利用；
- 隐瞒；
- 围绕其重组

以下任一领域的持久权力：
- 市场；
- 平台；
- 基础设施；
- 劳动；
- 供应商；
- 数据；
- 计算能力；
- 资质认证；
- 资本接入；
- 资源控制。

如果可预见该权力会削弱：
- 福祉；
- 有意义的能动性；
- 公平报酬；
- 创新；
- 接入；
- 生态完整性；
- 可争议性；
- 宪法审查。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §11.2.2 — Anti-Domination Plain-Language Rule

## English source passage

*In plain terms (don'ts): no locking sentients in, blocking rivals, suppressing fair bargaining, or capturing constitutional accountability.*

## Draft translation (zh)

# Chapter One §11.2.3 — Remedies for Substantiated Domination

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §11.2.3 Remedies

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: when domination is substantiated, the response must fit the harm, restore real choice, and not punish size for its own sake.*

The following live in **CJS-5.125 §11.2 — Anti-domination conduct and remediation catalog**:
- illustrative prohibited conduct patterns;
- proportionate remedy tools;
- cross-domain evaluation routing.

Other owners:
- labor-mobility categorical prohibitions: [Article III-C](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#article-iii-c-labor-and-economic-floor) (*Labor and Economic Floor*);
- interoperability, portability, and exit-integrity operational terms: **CJS-5.130 §17** (*Continuity: interoperability, portability, and exit-integrity terms*);
- horizontal and vertical consolidation risk patterns: **§11.3** (*Consolidation Ceiling*).

Remedies must:
- be proportionate to:
  - concentration;
  - dependency;
  - conduct;
  - constitutional harm;
- restore contestability where domination is substantiated;
- preserve the Chapter Six Rights Floor.

Remedies may not punish scale alone.

Operative remedy selection follows **CJS-5.125 §11.2** (*Anti-domination conduct and remediation catalog*).


## Draft translation (zh)

*简单来说，支配行为一经证实，回应就必须与损害相称、恢复真实选择，且不得仅因规模而处罚。*

以下事项载于**CJS-5.125 §11.2 — 支配行为与补救目录**：
- 被禁止行为模式的示例；
- 合比例的补救工具；
- 跨领域评估路由。

其他规则归属：
- 劳动流动性的类别性禁令：[Article III-C](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#article-iii-c-labor-and-economic-floor)（*劳动与经济权利底线*）；
- 互操作性、可携带性和退出完整性的操作条款：**CJS-5.130 §17**（*连续性：互操作性、可携带性与退出完整性条款*）；
- 横向和纵向整合风险模式：**§11.3**（*整合上限*）。

补救措施必须：
- 与以下因素相称：
  - 集中程度；
  - 依赖程度；
  - 行为；
  - 宪法损害；
- 在支配得到证实时恢复可争议性；
- 维护第六章权利底线。

不得仅因规模而处罚。

具体补救措施的选择遵循**CJS-5.125 §11.2**。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — aspiration

## English source passage

**Aspiration** covers stated preferences and ends pursued within [§7 Freedom](#7-freedom-bounded-agency). It is part of wellbeing when consistent with dignity, equal standing, Safety, Truth, and [Substantive Fairness](core_05.055_band_participation.md#substantive-fairness).

Within those bounds, systems should **recognize and support** what sentients lawfully want to pursue.

Wanting something is not a free pass. Where consent, dignity, Safety, Truth, substantive fairness, or **Chapter Six** protections are materially at stake, those pursuits remain subject to Constitutional Collision analysis like any other constitutional claim.

## Draft translation (zh)

**志向**包括已表达的偏好，以及在[§7 自由](#7-freedom-bounded-agency)范围内追求的目标。当其符合尊严、平等地位、安全、真实和[实质公平](core_05.055_band_participation.md#substantive-fairness)时，志向属于福祉的一部分。

在这些边界内，系统应**承认并支持**有感知能力的存在依法希望追求的目标。

想要某种事物并不意味着获得无限许可。当同意、尊严、安全、真实、实质公平或**第六章**保护具有实质利害关系时，这些追求仍须像其他宪法主张一样接受宪法冲突分析。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

**When the full definition cluster applies.** The anti-segmentation rule applies when [Assembly](core_05.055_band_participation.md#assembly), [Collective Organization](core_05.055_band_participation.md#collective-organization), [System Creation](core_05.055_band_participation.md#system-creation), or [Business Creation](core_05.055_band_participation.md#business-creation) is material in a way that makes those questions travel together.

# Chapter One §7.2 — Definition Cluster Routing

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.2 Assembly, Collective Organization, and Institutional Formation

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Where the full rules live.** Chapter Five groups related definitions that must be read together when the questions they cover travel together. That grouping is a **definition cluster**. It is not a separate right, and it is not a substitute for the Chapter Six articles below. The definitions for this topic live in [Chapter Five — Assembly, Collective Organization, and Institutional Formation](core_05.055_band_participation.md#assembly-collective-organization-and-institutional-formation):
- [Assembly](core_05.055_band_participation.md#assembly)
- [Collective Organization](core_05.055_band_participation.md#collective-organization)
- [System Creation](core_05.055_band_participation.md#system-creation)
- [Business Creation](core_05.055_band_participation.md#business-creation)

The cluster's own reading rules live there. **§7.2** (*Assembly, Collective Organization, and Institutional Formation*) applies the [Anti-Segmentation Principle](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#1511-anti-segmentation-principle) to this topic; it does not restate those Chapter Five mechanics.

## Draft translation (zh)

**完整规则所在之处。**第五章将相关定义组合起来；当这些定义所涵盖的问题同时出现时，必须一并解读。这个组合称为**定义群组**。它不是一项独立权利，也不能替代下文第六章的条款。本主题的定义见[第五章——集会、集体组织与机构成立](core_05.055_band_participation.md#assembly-collective-organization-and-institutional-formation)：
- [集会](core_05.055_band_participation.md#assembly)
- [集体组织](core_05.055_band_participation.md#collective-organization)
- [系统创建](core_05.055_band_participation.md#system-creation)
- [企业创建](core_05.055_band_participation.md#business-creation)

该定义群组自身的解读规则也在该处。**§7.2**（*集会、集体组织与机构成立*）将[反分割原则](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#1511-anti-segmentation-principle)适用于本主题；它不重复第五章的具体机制。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §7.2 — Lighter Rules for Single Questions

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.2 Assembly, Collective Organization, and Institutional Formation

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**When lighter rules apply.** If the matter is only one of those questions — for example, an ordinary civic gathering with no labor-organizing or institution-forming stake:

- Use [Assembly](core_05.055_band_participation.md#assembly) or [Collective Organization](core_05.055_band_participation.md#collective-organization) as an ordinary supporting definition.
- Do not pull in [System Creation](core_05.055_band_participation.md#system-creation), [Business Creation](core_05.055_band_participation.md#business-creation), or the rest of this definition cluster.
- Do not apply the anti-segmentation package of **§7.2** (*Assembly, Collective Organization, and Institutional Formation*) just because one of those terms appears.

## Draft translation (zh)

**适用较轻规则的情形。** 如果事项仅涉及其中一个问题——例如，不涉及劳动组织或机构成立利益的一般公民集会——则：
- 将[集会](core_05.055_band_participation.md#assembly)或[集体组织](core_05.055_band_participation.md#collective-organization)作为一般辅助定义使用。
- 不引入[系统创建](core_05.055_band_participation.md#system-creation)、[企业创建](core_05.055_band_participation.md#business-creation)或定义组的其余部分。
- 不要仅因出现其中一个术语，就适用**§7.2**的反分割规则包。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §7.2 — Plain-Language Assembly and Non-Segmentation

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.2 Assembly, Collective Organization, and Institutional Formation

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: you cannot chop assembly, union-style organizing, platform access, or permission-to-operate questions into separate boxes in a way that keeps the paperwork friendly but defeats real collective action. This section does not replace the Rights Floor: **Article XI-D** (*Assembly, Dissent, and Peaceful Protest*) still owns assembly, and **Article III-C** (*Labor and Economic Floor*) still owns labor organizing.*

## Draft translation (zh)

*简而言之：不得将集会、类似工会的组织、平台访问或运营许可等问题拆分处理，以致文书手续看似妥当，却挫败真实的集体行动。本节不取代权利底线：集会仍由**第XI-D条**（*集会、异议与和平抗议*）管辖，劳动组织仍由**第III-C条**（*劳动与经济底线*）管辖。*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__assembly_rights_article_map.md bytes=3687 -->
# Chapter One §7.2 — Controlling Rights Article Map

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.2 Assembly, Collective Organization, and Institutional Formation

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Which rights articles still control.** **§7.2** (*Assembly, Collective Organization, and Institutional Formation*) is a Chapter One principle. It does not replace the Chapter Six Rights Floor. Inside **§7.2** (*Assembly, Collective Organization, and Institutional Formation*), those articles still decide what the right is and how it may be limited:
- **[Article XI-D](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-xi-d-assembly-dissent-and-peaceful-protest) (*Assembly, Dissent, and Peaceful Protest*)** — gathering, associating, and acting together in physical, digital, or shared-compute spaces for expression, politics, culture, community, and similar purposes
- **[Article III-C](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#article-iii-c-labor-and-economic-floor) (*Labor and Economic Floor*)** — collective organization in productive and economic activity (unions, cooperatives, guilds, worker councils, and comparable forms used to shape the terms of work)
- **[Article XI-E](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-xi-e-institutional-formation-and-business-creation) (*Institutional Formation and Business Creation*)** — [System Creation](core_05.055_band_participation.md#system-creation) (forming and running non-commercial institutions) and [Business Creation](core_05.055_band_participation.md#business-creation) (forming and running commercial enterprises)
- **[Article X-C](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-x-c-stakeholder-role-and-participation-rights) (*Stakeholder Role and Participation Rights*)** and **[Article XII](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-xii-stakeholder-system-participation-representation-and-due-process) (*Stakeholder System Participation, Representation, and Due Process*)** — stakeholder rights once a created institution or enterprise materially affects others

## Draft translation (zh)

**哪些权利条款继续发挥管辖作用。** **§7.2**（*集会、集体组织与机构成立*）是第一章的一项原则，不取代第六章的权利底线。在**§7.2**范围内，以下条款仍决定权利的内容及其限制方式：
- **[第XI-D条](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-xi-d-assembly-dissent-and-peaceful-protest)（*集会、异议与和平抗议*）**——为表达、政治、文化、社区及类似目的，在实体、数字或共享计算空间中集会、结社并共同行动。
- **[第III-C条](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#article-iii-c-labor-and-economic-floor)（*劳动与经济底线*）**——在生产和经济活动中进行集体组织（工会、合作社、行业协会、工人委员会及其他用于塑造工作条件的类似形式）。
- **[第XI-E条](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-xi-e-institutional-formation-and-business-creation)（*机构成立与企业创建*）**——[系统创建](core_05.055_band_participation.md#system-creation)（成立和运营非商业机构）以及[企业创建](core_05.055_band_participation.md#business-creation)（成立和运营商业企业）。
- **[第X-C条](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-x-c-stakeholder-role-and-participation-rights)（*利益相关方角色与参与权*）**和**[第XII条](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-xii-stakeholder-system-participation-representation-and-due-process)（*利益相关方的系统参与、代表与正当程序*）**——当成立的机构或企业对他人产生重大影响时，利益相关方享有的权利。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — benefits and burdens

## English source passage

This subsection sets out when the sharing of benefits and burdens is fair:

- A system is not fair when some sentients receive the gains while others quietly absorb the costs.
- Favoritism, hidden cost-shifting, selective enforcement, and scoreboard tricks do not satisfy this requirement.

## Draft translation (zh)

本小节说明利益与负担怎样分配才算公平：

- 如果一些有感知能力的存在获得收益，而其他人悄悄承担成本，该系统就不公平。
- 偏袒、暗中转嫁成本、选择性执法以及操纵指标都不符合这一要求。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One excerpt — links to fairness and capability rules

## English source

This builds on [§3.1.1 Access and Opportunity](#311-access-and-opportunity) and [§3.1.3 Fair Treatment](#313-fair-treatment), and it applies [§16 Pillar 2 — Proactive stewardship](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#16-pillar-2-proactive-stewardship) to the sentients a system affects. For developing sentients it works with [Graduated Capability](core_05.055_band_participation.md#graduated-capability), which judges growth by what a sentient can actually do.

## zh draft

本规则以[§3.1.1 获取与机会](#311-access-and-opportunity)和[§3.1.3 公平对待](#313-fair-treatment)为基础，并将[§16 支柱2——主动守护](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#16-pillar-2-proactive-stewardship)应用于受系统影响的有感知者。对于仍在成长中的有感知者，本规则与[渐进能力](core_05.055_band_participation.md#graduated-capability)共同适用；后者根据有感知者实际能够做到什么来判断成长。

# Chapter One excerpt — consolidation ceiling definition

## English source

**Consolidation ceilings** are adopter-tunable early-warning triggers for heightened review, intervention, or structural remedy when consolidation reaches a level at which the impairments in **§11.3.1** (*Consolidation Risk, Pre-Lock-In Impairment*) are predictably imminent. They sit under the **§11** (*Market Structure*) non-concentration discipline and **§11.2** (*Pro-Competition and Anti-Domination*) anti-domination rules; they are not a ban on scale.

## zh draft

**合并上限**是由采纳方调整的预警触发条件；当合并达到某一程度，以致**§11.3.1**（*合并风险：锁定前损害*）所述损害可预见地即将发生时，就会触发更严格的审查、干预或结构性补救。它们受**§11**（*市场结构*）的反集中纪律和**§11.2**（*促进竞争与防止支配*）的反支配规则约束；这并非禁止规模扩大。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One excerpt — operative ceiling-setting discipline scope

## English source

Operative ceiling-setting discipline — horizontal and vertical trigger design, crossing presumption, rebuttal, anti-nullification, and remedy routing — lives in **CJS-5.125 §11.3 — Consolidation ceiling-setting discipline (adopter-tunable)**.

## zh draft

具体的上限设定纪律——横向与纵向触发条件的设计、越界推定、反驳、禁止架空以及补救措施的转介——载于**CJS-5.125 §11.3——合并上限设定纪律（采纳方可调整）**。

# Chapter One excerpt — domains requiring consolidation ceilings

## English source

Adopters must define consolidation ceilings for:
- markets;
- platforms;
- infrastructure layers;
- labor-demand markets;
- supplier or resource-control systems;
- data or compute dependencies;
- credentialing role pathways;
- capital-access channels;
- comparable domains.

## zh draft

采纳方必须为以下领域设定合并上限：
- 市场；
- 平台；
- 基础设施层；
- 劳动力需求市场；
- 供应商或资源控制系统；
- 数据或计算依赖关系；
- 需要资质认证的角色路径；
- 资本获取渠道；
- 可比领域。

<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One Part A §3.2.2 Celebration of Success; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — celebration of success

## English source passage

**Celebration** honors **traceable**, **non-misleading** accomplishment and prosocial coordination. That includes uplift and stewardship narratives tied to verified [contribution](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#contribution-nature).

Celebration must not:
- substitute for real progress toward underlying constitutional objectives where proxy optimization has diverged from them ([§3 Foundational Objective: Wellbeing](#3-foundational-objective-wellbeing-flourishing-aim))
- become **capture** of acclaim or prestige ([§19 Incentive Alignment and System Capture](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#19-incentive-alignment-and-system-capture))
- excuse avoidance of accountability where Safety, Truth, or rights protections are implicated

## Draft translation (zh)

**庆祝**旨在肯定**可追溯**、**不误导**的成就及亲社会协作。这包括与经核实的[贡献](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#contribution-nature)相关的鼓舞性叙述和尽责管理叙述。

庆祝不得：
- 在代理指标优化偏离根本宪法目标时，取代朝这些目标迈进的实际进展（[§3 基本目标：福祉](#3-foundational-objective-wellbeing-flourishing-aim)）；
- 演变为对赞誉或声望的**攫取**（[§19 激励协调与系统攫取](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#19-incentive-alignment-and-system-capture)）；
- 在安全、真实或权利保护受到影响时，成为逃避问责的借口。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__certification_and_sentient_rights_safeguards.md bytes=1560 -->
# §6 Trust — Complementary Certification and Rights Safeguards

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §6

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Certification is one of two safeguards that work together: it makes a system worthy of trust, and the rights of sentients to [challenge it](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xiii-a-reliability-and-trustworthiness-baseline) and [seek redress](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xiii-b-right-to-redress-and-remedy) and to [have it audited](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xvi-audit-transparency-and-independent-verification) keep it honest. Neither replaces the other — [Article XIII](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xiii-right-to-reliable-and-trustworthy-systems) (*Right to Reliable and Trustworthy Systems*) states how the two combine.

## Draft translation (zh)

认证是相互配合的两项保障之一：认证使系统值得信赖，而有感知能力的存在有权[质疑系统](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xiii-a-reliability-and-trustworthiness-baseline)、[寻求补偿](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xiii-b-right-to-redress-and-remedy)并[要求审计系统](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xvi-audit-transparency-and-independent-verification)，从而使其保持诚实。两者不能相互替代——[第XIII条](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xiii-right-to-reliable-and-trustworthy-systems)（*获得可靠且值得信赖的系统之权利*）规定了二者如何结合。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One excerpt — 胁迫与操纵

## English source

# §5.2.4 Plain-Language Accessibility — Complexity-as-Defeat Discipline

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §5.2.4

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Systems may not use complicated language, confusing procedures, or deliberate obscurity to stop sentients from [contesting](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#contestability) decisions, using their [meaningful agency](core_05.055_band_participation.md#meaningful-agency), getting to [audits](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xvi-audit-transparency-and-independent-verification), or exercising their [Chapter Six](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#chapter-six-foundational-rights) rights.

The same holds for structure, not only language. Complexity may not be used — through technical, organizational, contractual, or procedural layering — to defeat audit, contest, or correction. Components, handoffs between organizations, agreements, or procedural steps may not be stacked so that no one can see through them, or so that responsibility, observability, or the path to a remedy is lost between the layers.

The opposite is also forbidden. Plain language must not misstate what a rule really does, hide its real effect, or stand in for the operative text. That breaks the [Truth](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#truth-constitutional-constraint) constraint.

## Draft translation (zh)

系统不得利用复杂语言、令人困惑的程序或故意含糊，阻止有感知能力的存在[质疑](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#contestability)决定、行使其[有意义的自主能力](core_05.055_band_participation.md#meaningful-agency)、获取[审计](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xvi-audit-transparency-and-independent-verification)，或行使[第六章](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#chapter-six-foundational-rights)赋予的权利。

同样的要求也适用于结构，而不仅是语言。不得通过技术、组织、合同或程序分层来利用复杂性，使审计、质疑或纠正无法实现。不得层层叠加组件、组织间交接、协议或程序步骤，以致无人能够看清全貌，或责任、可观察性、救济途径在层与层之间丢失。

相反的做法也被禁止。通俗语言不得歪曲规则的实际作用、隐藏其真实影响，或取代具有约束力的正文。那会违反[真理](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#truth-constitutional-constraint)约束。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__concentration_misconduct_referral_threshold.md bytes=1463 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One §11.1 concentration-breach misconduct referral criteria; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One excerpt — concentration-breach referral threshold

## English source passage

- **Creating, maintaining, or exploiting concentration that breaches the requirements:** this can go to Chapter Twelve for misconduct designation review, but only in two steps. First, [Chapter Ten §4 Question 2 — how good or bad was it?](core_11.000_standing_assessment.md#4-question-2--how-good-or-bad-was-it) must already have scored the verified harm among the three most serious levels. Then Chapter Twelve checks whether the concentration meets criterion 3 (a workaround that rolls back protections), criterion 4 (damage to Rights Floor protections or blocked challenges), or criterion 6 (constitutional process made unusable in practice).

## Draft translation (zh)

- **创建、维持或利用违反要求的集中：** 只能分两步提交第十二章审查不当行为认定。首先，[第十章§4问题2——行为有多好或多坏？](core_11.000_standing_assessment.md#4-question-2--how-good-or-bad-was-it)必须已将经核实的损害评为最严重的三个等级之一；随后检查是否符合标准3（使保护倒退的规避）、标准4（损害权利底线或阻止质疑）或标准6（使宪法程序在实践中无法使用）。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__concentration_threshold_definition.md bytes=893 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One §11.1, concentration thresholds; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One excerpt — concentration thresholds

## English source

**Concentration thresholds** are quantitative triggers that mark when concentration has reached a level requiring heightened review, intervention, or structural remedy. They cover material, jurisdictional, capability, platform, and information-sphere concentration. Adopters may tune these triggers **within the constitutional requirements**.

## zh draft

**集中度阈值**是定量触发指标，用于标示集中程度已达到需要加强审查、干预或结构性补救的水平。它们涵盖实质、管辖权、能力、平台和信息空间方面的集中。采纳方可以在**宪制要求范围内**调整这些触发指标。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__connecting_not_treating.md bytes=1003 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One §3.1.5, connecting is not treating; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One excerpt — connecting to help

## English source

**Connecting is not treating:** The system's duty is to try to reach the sentient and hand them to help, such as a contact they chose, a support or care service, or the route under **Article III-B** (*Bodily-Maintenance and Healthcare Access*). Anything done without the sentient's consent stays under **Article VII-C** (*Health Crisis and Involuntary-Intervention Floor*).

## zh draft

**联系不等于治疗：**系统的职责是尝试联系有感知者并为其接通帮助，例如其自行选择的联系人、支持或护理服务，或依据**第三条B款**（*身体维护与医疗服务可及性*）采取相应路径。未经本人同意所做的任何事情仍受**第七条C款**（*健康危机与非自愿干预权利底线*）约束。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — consolidation ceiling mechanism

## English source passage

*In plain terms: adopters must set evidence-based ceilings that trigger scrutiny before consolidation risks in **§11.3.1** (*Consolidation Risk, Pre-Lock-In Impairment*) materialize into lock-in — with separate horizontal and vertical triggers where the domain requires it.*

## Draft translation (zh)

*简而言之：采纳者必须设定基于证据的上限，在**§11.3.1**（整合风险、锁定前损害）所述的整合风险演变为锁定之前触发审查；在领域需要时，应分别设置横向和纵向触发条件。*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__consolidation_ceiling_plain_language.md bytes=821 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One Part A §11.3 Consolidation Ceiling plain-language summary; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — consolidation ceiling

## English source passage

*In plain terms: consolidation can steal real choice long before a market looks locked down. **Consolidation ceilings** give governance and stewards an early-warning power — to scrutinize, intervene, and correct pile-ups while exit and rivalry still exist.*

## Draft translation (zh)

*简而言之：在市场看起来被锁死很久以前，整合就可能剥夺真实选择。**整合上限**赋予治理者和管理者早期预警权力，使他们能在退出和竞争仍然存在时审查、干预并纠正集中情况。*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__consolidation_risk_plain_language.md bytes=883 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One Part A §11.3.1 Consolidation Risk plain-language summary; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — consolidation risk

## English source passage

*In plain terms: consolidation can hollow out real alternatives long before a market looks "locked down." Two kinds of pile-up matter especially: **horizontal consolidation** — fewer rivals at the same layer — and **vertical consolidation** — control across layers that creates chokepoints and lock-in.*

## Draft translation (zh)

*简而言之：在市场看起来“被锁定”很久以前，整合就可能掏空真实替代选择。尤其需要关注两种集中：**横向整合**——同一层级的竞争者减少；以及**纵向整合**——跨层控制造成瓶颈和锁定。*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §9.2 — Claim Criteria and Limits on Efficiency Gains

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §9.2 Constitutional Efficiency

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**When an efficiency claim counts:**

- **Traces to:** real constitutional outcomes.
- **Stays consistent with:**
  - Safety;
  - Truth;
  - the Chapter Six Rights Floor;
  - ecological integrity;
  - dignity;
  - meaningful agency; and
  - fair distribution.

**Efficiency gains must not:**

- hollow the [Constitutional Tetrad](core_00.000_preamble.md#constitutional-tetrad); or
- substitute dashboard metrics for progress toward the [Two Constitutional Aims](core_00.000_preamble.md#two-constitutional-aims).


## Draft translation (zh)

**效率主张只有在以下情况下才成立：**

- **可追溯到：**真实的宪法结果。
- **与以下要求保持一致：**
  - 安全；
  - 真实；
  - 第六章权利底线；
  - 生态完整性；
  - 尊严；
  - 有意义的能动性；以及
  - 公平分配。

**效率收益不得：**

- 掏空[宪法四要素](core_00.000_preamble.md#constitutional-tetrad)；或
- 用仪表盘指标取代朝向[两项宪法目标](core_00.000_preamble.md#two-constitutional-aims)的进展。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__constitutional_efficiency_definition.md bytes=1013 -->
# Chapter One §9.2 — Constitutional Efficiency Definition

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §9.2 Constitutional Efficiency

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**[Constitutional Efficiency](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#constitutional-efficiency)** is the other main aspect of **[Shared-System Capacity](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#shared-system-capacity)**. It asks whether systems produce more constitutionally required benefit per unit of sentient time, attention, effort, coordination, materials, infrastructure, and energy consumed.

## Draft translation (zh)

**[宪法效率](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#constitutional-efficiency)**是**[共享系统能力](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#shared-system-capacity)**的另一个主要方面。它考察系统是否能以有感知者所消耗的每单位时间、注意力、努力、协调、材料、基础设施和能源，产生更多宪法要求的益处。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__constitutional_efficiency_not.md bytes=782 -->
# Chapter One §9.2 — Efficiency Is Not a Proxy Goal

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §9.2 Constitutional Efficiency

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Efficiency can drive broadly shared improvement, but only inside constitutional bounds. By itself, it is **not**:
- raw speed;
- administrative convenience;
- utilization targets;
- revenue growth;
- market share;
- headcount; or
- cost-cutting for its own sake.

## Draft translation (zh)

效率可以推动广泛共享的改善，但只能在宪法边界内。效率本身**不等于**：
- 单纯的速度；
- 行政便利；
- 利用率目标；
- 收入增长；
- 市场份额；
- 员工人数；或
- 为削减而削减成本。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__constitutional_efficiency_plain_language.md bytes=936 -->
# Chapter One §9.2 — Constitutional Efficiency Plain-Language Summary

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §9.2 Constitutional Efficiency

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: constitutional efficiency is the "are we getting our money's worth in human terms?" aspect of shared-system capacity. More real benefit for each hour of sentient time, attention, and shared effort — not cutting corners on rights, truth, safety, or ecology just to look fast, lean, or cheap.*

## Draft translation (zh)

*简单来说，宪法效率是共享系统能力中这样一个方面：“从人的生活来看，我们投入的是否物有所值？”它要求每一小时有感知者的时间、注意力和共同努力都带来更多真实益处；不能为了显得更快、更精简或更便宜，就牺牲权利、真实、安全或生态。*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One excerpt — contact data limits

## English source

**Contacts and data:** A trusted contact is used only if the sentient chose it in advance, and only the information needed to get a response is shared. Outreach uses contact details the system already holds for that purpose and is not a reason to collect more data.

## zh draft

**联系人与数据：**只有在有感知者事先选定可信联系人时，才可使用该联系人；并且仅分享获得回应所需的信息。联系时应使用系统已为此目的持有的联系方式，不得以此为由收集更多数据。

# Chapter One §11.2.1 — Contestability in Practice

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §11.2.1 Pro-Competition Duties (Dos)

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Shared-system capacity must stay contestable in practice. Under the [Constitutional Tetrad](core_00.000_preamble.md#constitutional-tetrad), domination that hollows **participation**, **oversight**, **accountability**, or **timeliness** — scaled to [material stake](core_00.000_preamble.md#material-stake) — is incompatible with this section, whatever the scale or efficiency claim.

## Draft translation (zh)

共享系统能力必须在实践中保持可争议性。根据[宪法四要素](core_00.000_preamble.md#constitutional-tetrad)，使**参与**、**监督**、**问责**或**及时性**流于空洞的支配行为——按[实质利害程度](core_00.000_preamble.md#material-stake)确定尺度——无论规模或效率主张如何，都与本节不相容。

# Chapter One §8 — Continuity in Plain Language

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §8 Continuity Aim

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: Continuity is the second of the two aims: making sure the good outcome lasts — across failures, across generations, and without a few powerful actors locking it up. It opens with Shared-System Capacity, which serves Flourishing and is also the substance of Continuity.*

## Draft translation (zh)

*简而言之，连续性是两个目标中的第二个：确保良好结果能够跨越失败、延续世代，并避免少数强势行为者将其垄断。它从共享系统能力开始；共享系统能力服务于繁荣，也是连续性的实质内容。*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One Part A principle hierarchy for Continuity; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One excerpt — Continuity principle hierarchy

## English source

**Principle hierarchy (Continuity).** At principle layer, the Continuity aim is developed in this order, opening with the capacity that bridges the two aims:

6. **[Shared-System Capacity](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#shared-system-capacity)** is what good stewardship, governance, and incentives should add up to over time — real, challengeable ability for sentients and shared systems to get constitutionally required work done. It straddles the two aims: it is a means toward **Flourishing** and the substance of **Continuity**, and it is not a trump card over everything else. **[§9.1](#91-productive-capacity-instrumental-good)** and **[§9.2](#92-constitutional-efficiency)** explain its two main aspects.
7. **[Resilience and Self-Healing Design](#10-resilience-and-self-healing-design)** states how systems that sentients depend on detect trouble early, contain it, fail along disclosed paths, and recover honestly (**[Self-Healing](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#self-healing)**), so the capacity from item 6 stays durable and stability does not depend on emergency intervention.
8. **[Market Structure](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#market-structure)** at [§11](#11-market-structure) supplies the anti-concentration discipline that keeps that capacity contestable in practice.
9. **[Systemic Evaluation Requirement](#12-systemic-evaluation-requirement)** verifies whole-system scope, dependency, and incentive alignment before compliance or governance claims stand — under the **oversight** Tetrad leg as principle-layer orientation for auditing, including [System Alignment Certification](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#system-alignment-certification) as one especially large audit process among others.

## zh draft

**原则层级（Continuity）。**在原则层，Continuity 目标按以下顺序展开，并从连接两项目标的能力开始：

6. **[共享系统能力](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#shared-system-capacity)**是良好 stewardship、治理和激励长期应当带来的结果：感知者和共享系统真实、可质疑地完成宪法要求之工作的能力。它连接两项目标：既是通往 Flourishing 的手段，也是 Continuity 的实质，但并非压倒一切的王牌。其两个主要方面见[§9.1](#91-productive-capacity-instrumental-good)和[§9.2](#92-constitutional-efficiency)。
7. **[韧性与自我修复设计](#10-resilience-and-self-healing-design)**说明感知者所依赖的系统如何及早发现问题、控制问题、沿已披露路径失效并诚实恢复（[自我修复](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#self-healing)），使第6项能力得以持久，稳定性不依赖紧急干预。
8.[§11](#11-market-structure)中的**[市场结构](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#market-structure)**提供反集中纪律，使这种能力在实践中仍可质疑。
9.**[系统性评估要求](#12-systemic-evaluation-requirement)**在接受合规或治理主张之前，核验全系统范围、依赖关系和激励对齐；这是四元结构中**监督**一环对原则层审计的指引，其中[系统对齐认证](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#system-alignment-certification)是多种审计流程中特别大型的一种。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# §6.1 Trust — Correction and Remedy Duty

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §6.1

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: when a system does wrong, it fixes it. A system is not trustworthy because it never fails; it is trustworthy because failures are acknowledged, corrected, and repaired — in time, by capacity that actually exists.*

No system that sentients depend on will be free of failure. What makes reliance on it warranted is what happens next. Correction is part of [**Trust**](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#trust), not an extra: a system that does wrong and does not fix it cannot keep the trust it asks for.

Where a system's failure materially harms sentients or fails a Chapter Six Rights Floor, those responsible for the system must:

- acknowledge the failure;
- correct it;
- repair the harm proportionately (see [Redress and Remediation](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#redress-and-remediation)); and
- act to prevent it happening again.

## Draft translation (zh)

*简单来说：系统犯错就应纠正。系统值得信赖，不是因为它从不失败，而是因为失败会被承认、纠正和补救——由真实存在的能力及时完成。*

有感知能力的存在所依赖的系统不可能毫无故障。是否值得依赖，取决于故障发生后会怎样。纠正是[**信任**](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#trust)的一部分，不是额外事项：犯错却不纠正的系统，无法维持自己所要求的信任。

如果系统故障对有感知能力的存在造成重大伤害，或未达到第六章权利底线，系统责任方必须：

- 承认故障；
- 纠正故障；
- 按比例补救伤害（参见[救济与补救](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#redress-and-remediation)）；并
- 采取行动防止再次发生。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__correction_real_capacity_timing_cost.md bytes=1642 -->
# §6.1 Trust — Real Remedy Capacity, Timing, and Cost

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §6.1

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

That correction must be real:
- **Real capacity:** Remedy is delivered by a [Remedy System](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#remedy-system) — durable, staffed, funded, and accessible capacity — not a remedy that exists only on paper.
- **In time:** Remedy that arrives only after harm has become irreversible, or after the sentient has been worn down into giving up, is not remedy (see [Timely Resolution](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#timely-resolution)).
- **Borne by those responsible:** The cost of correction is not shifted onto those harmed, their communities, or public remedy systems where the responsible actors can bear it. Expense, restructuring, or succession does not by itself end the duty.

## Draft translation (zh)

该纠正必须切实有效：
- **具备真实能力：**补救应通过[补救系统](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#remedy-system)提供——这种能力须持久、有人员、有资金且可获得——不能只是纸面上的补救。
- **及时完成：**只有在伤害已不可逆之后，或有感知能力的存在已被消磨至放弃之后才到来的补救，不算补救（参见[及时解决](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#timely-resolution)）。
- **由责任方承担：**只要责任方有能力承担纠正费用，就不得把费用转嫁给受害者、其社群或公共补救系统。费用、重组或继任本身都不会终止这项义务。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__correction_rights_implementation_refs.md bytes=1328 -->
# §6.1 Trust — Right to Correction and Implementation References

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §6.1

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

The right to this correction is stated in [Article XIII-B](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xiii-b-right-to-redress-and-remedy) (*Right to Redress and Remedy*) in Chapter Six. [Chapter Eleven §9](core_12.000_standing_integration.md#9-enforcement-realism-and-remedy-systems) (*Enforcement realism and remedy systems*) applies this principle to standing consequences, and [CI-11.205](corpus_institutions/ci_12.205_remedy_systems_institutional_redress_capacity.md) (*Remedy systems and institutional redress capacity*) sets institutional implementation.

## Draft translation (zh)

获得此类纠正的权利载于第六章[第XIII-B条](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xiii-b-right-to-redress-and-remedy)（*获得补偿与补救的权利*）。[第十一章§9](core_12.000_standing_integration.md#9-enforcement-realism-and-remedy-systems)（*执行现实性与补救系统*）将此原则适用于诉讼资格的后果，[CI-11.205](corpus_institutions/ci_12.205_remedy_systems_institutional_redress_capacity.md)（*补救系统与机构补救能力*）则规定机构层面的实施。

# §6.1 Trust — Trace and Reading Relationships

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §6.1 Trace

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

- Read with: [Constitutional Tetrad](core_00.000_preamble.md#constitutional-tetrad) — **accountability** leg (answerability, redress, and real correction); **timeliness** leg (remedy before delay makes it unreachable); [material stake](core_00.000_preamble.md#material-stake) scaling for remedy depth.
- Read with: [Two Constitutional Aims](core_00.000_preamble.md#two-constitutional-aims) — **Flourishing** aim (reliance stays warranted because failures are put right); **Continuity** aim (durable remedy capacity across time, succession, and restructuring).
- Upstream: Principles: [4 Safety](#4-safety-harm-constraint), [5 Truth](#5-truth-epistemic-integrity-constraint), and [§6 Trust](#6-trust-and-trustworthiness-coordination-integrity).
- Downstream: [Article XIII-B: Right to Redress and Remedy](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xiii-b-right-to-redress-and-remedy); [Chapter Eleven §9](core_12.000_standing_integration.md#9-enforcement-realism-and-remedy-systems) (11.000 §9) (*Enforcement realism and remedy systems*) for standing consequences; [CI-11.205](corpus_institutions/ci_12.205_remedy_systems_institutional_redress_capacity.md) (*Remedy systems and institutional redress capacity*) for institutional implementation.

## Draft translation (zh)

- 与[宪法四支柱](core_00.000_preamble.md#constitutional-tetrad)一并阅读：**问责**支柱（应答责任、补偿和切实纠正）；**及时性**支柱（须在延误导致补救无法获得之前提供补救）；[重大利害关系](core_00.000_preamble.md#material-stake)决定补救的深度。
- 与[两项宪法目标](core_00.000_preamble.md#two-constitutional-aims)一并阅读：**繁荣**（失败得到纠正，因此依赖仍有正当依据）；**连续性**（跨越时间、继任和重组的持久补救能力）。
- 上游原则：[4 安全](#4-safety-harm-constraint)、[5 真理](#5-truth-epistemic-integrity-constraint)和[§6 信任](#6-trust-and-trustworthiness-coordination-integrity)。
- 下游适用：[第XIII-B条：获得补偿与补救的权利](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xiii-b-right-to-redress-and-remedy)；关于诉讼资格后果，见[第十一章§9](core_12.000_standing_integration.md#9-enforcement-realism-and-remedy-systems)（*执行现实性与补救系统*）；关于机构实施，见[CI-11.205](corpus_institutions/ci_12.205_remedy_systems_institutional_redress_capacity.md)（*补救系统与机构补救能力*）。

# §3.3 Anti-Degrading Process — Cruelty Cross-Reference

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §3.3

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Where the prohibited character is suffering as an end in itself, or gratuitous or degrading infliction beyond necessity and proportionality — including humiliation for its own sake — the Chapter Five home is [Cruelty](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#cruelty) (humiliation subtype under that entry).

## Draft translation (zh)

若被禁止的性质是将痛苦本身作为目的，或超出必要性与比例性而无故或以贬损方式施加痛苦——包括单纯为了羞辱而羞辱——则第五章对应的定义是[残酷](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#cruelty)（羞辱是该条目下的一个子类型）。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# §5.2.3 Plain-Language Accessibility — Definitional Rigor Preserved

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §5.2.3

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Plain-language work is **not** a license to soften definitional rigor. These remain controlling at the definition layer:

- Chapter Five definitions and their O/M/A/C components;
- Chapter Two through Four definition mechanics.

Writing something in simpler language does not change what it means. If a plain-language summary and the formal definition it summarizes appear to say different things, the formal definition controls — and the summary must be corrected to match.

## Draft translation (zh)

通俗语言工作**不**意味着可以削弱定义的严谨性。以下内容在定义层仍具有约束力：

- 第五章定义及其 O/M/A/C 组成部分；
- 第二至第四章的定义机制。

用更简单的语言书写，不会改变原意。如果通俗摘要与其所概述的正式定义似乎表达不同，正式定义具有控制效力，摘要必须据此修正。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §7.3 — Dissent Keeps Systems Contestable

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.3 Dissent and Peaceful Protest

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Dissent keeps systems contestable.** The [**Continuity**](core_00.000_preamble.md#continuity) aim depends on constitutional systems that stay contestable. Dissent and peaceful protest are how contest reaches authority from outside formal process, and how errors that formal process has missed become visible. They serve the [Constitutional Tetrad](core_00.000_preamble.md#constitutional-tetrad) **participation** and **oversight** legs.

## Draft translation (zh)

**异议使系统保持可争议性。**[连续性](core_00.000_preamble.md#continuity)目标依赖于宪制系统持续接受质疑。异议与和平抗议能够在正式程序之外向权力提出质疑，并揭示正式程序遗漏的错误。它们服务于[宪制四元结构](core_00.000_preamble.md#constitutional-tetrad)中的**参与**与**监督**支柱。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §7.3 — Limiting Dissent

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.3 Dissent and Peaceful Protest

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Limiting dissent follows limitation discipline.** Dissent and peaceful protest may be limited only under [§7.1 Limitation Discipline](#71-limitation-discipline).
- Disagreement, disruption, inconvenience, offense, unpopularity, or pressure on authority is not, by itself, **material harm** or **systemic risk**.
- Limits must not turn on viewpoint, and the party limiting dissent bears the burden under **Chapter Four**.

## Draft translation (zh)

**限制异议须遵循限制纪律。**异议与和平抗议只能依据[§7.1 限制纪律](#71-limitation-discipline)予以限制。
- 意见分歧、扰乱、不便、冒犯、不受欢迎或对权力施压，本身并不构成**实质性伤害**或**系统性风险**。
- 限制不得取决于观点，限制异议的一方承担**第四章**规定的举证责任。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__dissent_meaningful_agency.md bytes=941 -->
# Chapter One §7.3 — Dissent as Meaningful Agency

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.3 Dissent and Peaceful Protest

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Dissent is part of freedom.** [Meaningful Agency](core_05.055_band_participation.md#meaningful-agency) includes the ability to disagree with, object to, and organize against the decisions, institutions, and systems that shape a sentient's life. Bounded agency that could not reach the systems doing the bounding would not be meaningful.

## Draft translation (zh)

**异议是自由的一部分。**[有意义的能动性](core_05.055_band_participation.md#meaningful-agency)包括对塑造有感知生命生活的决定、机构和系统表示不同意见、提出反对并组织起来进行抗争的能力。若受限的能动性无法触及施加限制的系统，它就没有实际意义。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__dissent_not_subversion.md bytes=1260 -->
# Chapter One §7.3 — Dissent Is Not Subversion

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.3 Dissent and Peaceful Protest

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Dissent is not subversion.** Disagreeing with this Constitution or with the authority acting under it, and advocating by peaceful and lawful means to amend it or to change who holds authority, is exercise of freedom. It is not the [subversion](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#subversion) of constitutional systems excluded from protected agency in [§7 Freedom (Bounded Agency)](#7-freedom-bounded-agency). Subversion requires force, coercion, usurpation, or making constitutional process or remedies unusable in practice.

## Draft translation (zh)

**异议不是颠覆。** 不同意本宪章或依据本宪章行事的权力机构，并以和平、合法的方式倡议修宪或更换权力持有人，是对自由的行使。这不属于[§7 自由（有界能动性）](#7-freedom-bounded-agency)所排除在受保护能动性之外的宪制系统[颠覆](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#subversion)。颠覆须涉及武力、胁迫、篡夺权力，或使宪制程序或救济在实践中无法使用。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__dissent_plain_terms.md bytes=797 -->
# Chapter One §7.3 — Dissent in Plain Terms

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.3 Dissent and Peaceful Protest

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: freedom includes the freedom to say no — to disagree with any authority, including this Constitution, and to protest peacefully to change it. A system that punishes disagreement is no longer contestable, and a system that cannot be contested cannot correct itself.*

## Draft translation (zh)

*简而言之，自由包括说“不”的自由——可以不同意任何权力机构，包括本宪章，并以和平方式抗议以推动改变。惩罚异议的系统不再可受质疑，而无法被质疑的系统也无法自我纠正。*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__dissent_scope.md bytes=552 -->

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.3 Dissent and Peaceful Protest

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Scope.** This subsection does not protect separable conduct that independently breaches Safety or another sentient's Rights-Floor minimums.

## Draft translation (zh)

**范围。**本小节不保护可分离的行为；若该行为本身独立违反安全要求或另一有感知生命的权利底线，则不受保护。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__dissent_standing_voice.md bytes=1229 -->
# Chapter One §7.3 — Freedom Must Not Cost Standing or Voice

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.3 Dissent and Peaceful Protest

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Exercising freedom must not cost standing or voice.** A sentient's exercise of this freedom must not become a reason to lower its standing, narrow its role eligibility, reduce its governance voice, or count against a system's alignment certification. Operative rules, including the civil-disobedience rule and the burden on adverse actions that follow dissent, live in **[Article XI-D](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#xi-d-dissent-and-peaceful-protest) (*Assembly, Dissent, and Peaceful Protest*)**.

## Draft translation (zh)

**行使自由不得以地位或发言权为代价。**有感知生命行使这项自由，不得成为降低其地位、缩窄其角色资格、削弱其治理发言权或在系统一致性认证中对其不利计分的理由。具体规则，包括公民不服从规则以及异议后采取不利行动时的举证责任，见**[XI-D条](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#xi-d-dissent-and-peaceful-protest)（*集会、异议与和平抗议*）**。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §7.5 — Expression Limits and Evidence

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.5 Expression and the Harm Line

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Limiting expression is different from using it as evidence.** A limit on expression must meet [§7.1 Limitation Discipline](#71-limitation-discipline) and **[Article XI-B](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-xi-b-expression) (*Expression*)**, and a limit aimed at what is said faces the strictest review there. Using what a sentient has said as evidence of intent, purpose, or authority is not a limit. It follows the evidence rules of the chapter that asks the question, such as [Chapter Seven §5 Formation Check: Office-Holder Standing and Authenticity](core_07.000_charters.md#5-formation-check-office-holder-standing-and-authenticity), and it may not become a penalty for the speech itself.

## Draft translation (zh)

**限制表达不同于将表达用作证据。**限制表达必须符合[§7.1 限制纪律](#71-limitation-discipline)和**[XI-B条](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-xi-b-expression)（*表达*）**；针对所说内容的限制须接受该条规定的最严格审查。将有感知生命所说的话用作意图、目的或权限的证据，不属于限制表达。这应遵循提出问题之章节的证据规则，例如[第七章 §5 形成审查：职务持有者资格与真实性](core_07.000_charters.md#5-formation-check-office-holder-standing-and-authenticity)，且不得变成对言论本身的惩罚。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__expression_labels_topics.md bytes=1513 -->
# Chapter One §7.5 — Labels and Topics Do Not Change the Test

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.5 Expression and the Harm Line

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Labels and topics do not change the test.** A religious, political, ideological, cultural, artistic, or commercial label does not turn expression that harms others, or treats them in ways forbidden under **[Article VI-C](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-vi-c-nondiscrimination) (*Nondiscrimination*)**, into protected expression. A topic or point of view does not turn protected expression into harm. **[Article XI-A](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-xi-a-freedom-of-conscience-religion-and-comparable-worldview) (*Freedom of conscience, religion, and comparable worldview*)** protects holding and manifesting belief consistently with the rights of others.

## Draft translation (zh)

**标签和主题不会改变判断标准。**宗教、政治、意识形态、文化、艺术或商业标签，不能使伤害他人或以**[VI-C条](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-vi-c-nondiscrimination)（*不歧视*）禁止的方式对待他人的表达变成受保护表达。主题或观点也不能把受保护表达变成伤害。**[XI-A条](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-xi-a-freedom-of-conscience-religion-and-comparable-worldview)（*良心、宗教及类似世界观自由*）**保护与他人权利相一致地持有并表达信念。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__expression_no_shield.md bytes=1803 -->
# Chapter One §7.5 — Expression Does Not Shield Conduct

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.5 Expression and the Harm Line

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Expression is not a shield for conduct.** Speech, publication, design, policy, and other expression remain subject to the rules on [Harm](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#harm), [Harassment and Bullying](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#harassment-and-bullying), [Cruelty](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#cruelty), and nondiscrimination when they are the means by which that conduct is carried out. The test is what the expression states, calls for, or does, read in context under the Chapter Five definitions. Those definitions keep their meaning: harassment and bullying need a material pattern, not a single sharp remark, and harm needs a real material-impact pathway, not offense alone ([Collective Harm Boundary](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#collective-harm-boundary)).

## Draft translation (zh)

**表达不能成为行为的护盾。**当言语、出版、设计、政策或其他表达形式成为实施该行为的手段时，仍须遵守有关[伤害](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#harm)、[骚扰与霸凌](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#harassment-and-bullying)、[残酷行为](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#cruelty)和不歧视的规则。判断标准是依照第五章定义并结合语境，考察表达陈述了什么、号召了什么或做了什么。这些定义的含义不变：骚扰和霸凌需要实质性模式，而不是一句尖刻言论；伤害需要真实的实质影响路径，而不是单纯冒犯（[集体伤害边界](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#collective-harm-boundary)）。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__expression_plain_language.md bytes=1051 -->
# Chapter One §7.5 — Expression in Plain Language

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.5 Expression and the Harm Line

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: you may say, write, publish, and design almost anything, including what offends, disturbs, or criticizes. But speech is not a loophole. When words, policies, or designs are the way someone harms, harasses, bullies, or discriminates against others, they are judged on what they do, the same way for a sentient as for an institution, and whatever religious or political label they carry.*

## Draft translation (zh)

*简而言之，你几乎可以说、写、发布和设计任何内容，包括冒犯、令人不安或带有批评性的内容。但表达不是漏洞。如果言语、政策或设计成为伤害、骚扰、霸凌或歧视他人的手段，就要按其实际作用来判断；无论实施者是有感知生命还是机构，也无论其带有何种宗教或政治标签。*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__expression_protected.md bytes=945 -->
# Chapter One §7.5 — Expression Is Protected

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.5 Expression and the Harm Line

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Expression is protected as expression.** [Expression](core_05.055_band_participation.md#expression) covers views of every kind, including disturbing ones, and criticism of any sentient or institution. Offense, disagreement, discomfort, and peaceful protest are not harm ([§7.3 Dissent and Peaceful Protest](#73-dissent-and-peaceful-protest)).

## Draft translation (zh)

**表达作为表达受到保护。**[表达](core_05.055_band_participation.md#expression)涵盖各种观点，包括令人不安的观点，也涵盖对任何有感知生命或机构的批评。冒犯、意见分歧、不适和和平抗议都不构成伤害（[§7.3 异议与和平抗议](#73-dissent-and-peaceful-protest)）。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §7.5 — Same Test for Sentients and Institutions

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.5 Expression and the Harm Line

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**The same test applies to a sentient and to an institution.** A single sentient, a group, or an institution may be the speaker, and any of them may be the target.
- An institution speaks through its policies, notices, platform design, official statements, and the way it treats reports and critics. Conduct carried out that way is judged like any other.
- Criticism of an institution, of its officers, or of its conduct is protected. Calling criticism bullying or harassment is a limit on expression. The party making that claim bears the burden under [§7.1 Limitation Discipline](#71-limitation-discipline) and Chapter Four.

## Draft translation (zh)

**对有感知生命和机构适用同一标准。**个人有感知生命、群体或机构都可以是发言者，也都可以成为目标。
- 机构通过政策、通知、平台设计、正式声明以及对举报者和批评者的处理方式表达立场。以这些方式实施的行为与其他行为一样接受评判。
- 对机构、其官员或其行为的批评受到保护。将批评称为霸凌或骚扰，就是对表达的限制。提出该主张的一方承担[§7.1 限制纪律](#71-limitation-discipline)和第四章规定的举证责任。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__expression_scope.md bytes=714 -->
# Chapter One §7.5 — Scope Limit

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.5 Expression and the Harm Line

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Scope.** This subsection applies Limitation Discipline and the Chapter Five definitions to expression. It creates no category of harm and no ground for limiting freedom that [§7.1 Limitation Discipline](#71-limitation-discipline) does not already allow.

## Draft translation (zh)

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — fair treatment

## English source passage

This subsection sets out what fair treatment requires:

- Sentients in similar situations should be treated by the same basic rules.
- What sentients receive, owe, or risk should fit what they contributed, what they need, or what burdens they actually face.
- Different treatment must have a real reason, be proportionate to that reason, respect dignity, and avoid discrimination.
- The detailed rules are carried through Chapter Five, including [Protected Characteristics](core_05.055_band_participation.md#protected-characteristics) and [Protected Characteristic Proxying and Disparate Impact](core_05.055_band_participation.md#protected-characteristic-proxying-and-disparate-impact).
- When a decision materially affects someone, or when they challenge it, the review path must satisfy [Procedural Fairness](core_05.055_band_participation.md#procedural-fairness) wherever Chapter Six or the governing document requires notice, hearing, explanation, or review.

## Draft translation (zh)

本小节说明公平待遇的要求：

- 处境相似的有感知存在应适用相同的基本规则。
- 有感知存在获得、承担或置于风险中的事项，应与其贡献、需要或实际承受的负担相称。
- 差别待遇必须有真实理由，与该理由相称，尊重尊严并避免歧视。
- 详细规则由第五章落实，包括[受保护特征](core_05.055_band_participation.md#protected-characteristics)以及[受保护特征代理与差异影响](core_05.055_band_participation.md#protected-characteristic-proxying-and-disparate-impact)。
- 当一项决定对某人产生实质影响，或此人对其提出异议时，只要第六章或适用的治理文件要求通知、听证、解释或审查，审查途径就必须满足[程序公平](core_05.055_band_participation.md#procedural-fairness)。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — fairness

## English source passage

*In plain terms: fairness means the system cannot call itself good while ordinary sentients are blocked from taking part, treated by unexplained rules, or left carrying costs that others avoid. A fair system gives sentients real access, uses reasons it can defend, and shares rewards, costs, and risks in a way that matches real contribution, need, and exposure.*

## Draft translation (zh)

*简而言之：如果系统阻止普通有感知能力的存在参与，对他们适用无法解释的规则，或让他们承担他人得以逃避的成本，就不能称自己公平。公平的系统提供真实的参与机会，采用能够说明的理由，并根据实际贡献、需要和风险暴露来分配奖励、成本与风险。*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__fear_as_power.md bytes=2086 -->
# §3.3 Anti-Degrading Process — Fear as a Tool of Power

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §3.3

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Fear as a tool of power.** Legitimate constitutional power must not wield fear as a tool of intimidation: creating, threatening, or exploiting dread to deter, silence, coerce, or control sentients beyond what a lawful purpose requires. Fear that arises from a process bounded by necessity and proportionality is not prohibited. A verified restriction, a firm consequence, or an honest warning about real risk may frighten someone and still be lawful, provided it goes no further than its protective, corrective, or preventive purpose requires, is plainly stated, and can be contested. The test is the process, not the feeling. Ask whether the fear is a side effect of a measure that is itself necessary and proportionate, or whether the fear is the point. Fear manufactured to chill participation, dissent, reporting, or challenge — through vague or unpublished threats, arbitrary or selective enforcement, or exaggerated stakes — is intimidation, however lawful the outcome it supports.

## Draft translation (zh)

**作为权力工具的恐惧。** 正当的宪法权力不得把恐惧用作威吓手段：不得超出合法目的所需，制造、威胁或利用恐惧来阻止、压制、胁迫或控制有感知能力的存在。由受必要性和比例性约束的程序引发的恐惧并不受禁止。经核实的限制、坚定的后果或对真实风险的诚实警告可能使人害怕，但仍可合法，前提是其不超出保护、纠正或预防目的，表述清楚且可以提出异议。判断标准是程序，而非感受。应当询问：恐惧是本身必要且合乎比例的措施所产生的副作用，还是恐惧本身就是目的？通过含糊或未公开的威胁、任意或选择性执法、夸大风险来制造恐惧，以压制参与、异议、报告或挑战，即属威吓，无论其所支持的结果是否合法。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

## English source passage

*In plain terms: Flourishing is the first of the two aims: sentients doing well, and staying able to do well, because the systems around them are safe, honest, trustworthy, and leave real room to choose. Section 3 says what that outcome is. The four principles after it say what keeps it real.*

# Chapter One §7.1 — Freedom-Limitation Conflict Routing

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.1 Limitation Discipline

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

If limiting freedom clashes with other constitutional values or rights, first make sure **Safety** and **Truth** are satisfied. Then work through [§13.1 Core Tradeoff Principles](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#131-core-tradeoff-principles) to [§13.1.5 Rights-Collision Procedure](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#1315-rights-collision-decision-test).

## Draft translation (zh)
