<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__freedom_last_resort_criteria.md bytes=2230 -->
# Chapter One §7.1 — Last-Resort Freedom-Limitation Criteria

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.1 Limitation Discipline

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

You may limit someone's freedom only as a last resort. If a less restrictive option would still prevent the material harm or systemic risk, you must use it. Every limit must meet all of these conditions:

- **Necessary:** No less restrictive option would still prevent the harm or risk. This is the [Necessity](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#necessity) test in Chapter Five.
- **Proven:** Anyone who says a limit is unavoidable must be able to show it, under the burden and traceability rules in Chapter Four. Convenience, cost alone, operator-created barriers, and habit do not count as proof.
- **Consistent with the definitions:** The claim must fit the Chapter Five definitions of Feasibility, Necessity, Proportionality, and Harm Minimization (Tradeoff Selection).
- **Overseen:** Every limit stays under [Oversight](core_05.020_apex_oversight_leg.md#oversight-constitutional), and the more is at stake, the closer that oversight must be ([material stake](core_00.000_preamble.md#material-stake)).

## Draft translation (zh)

只有作为最后手段，才可以限制他人的自由。如果限制较少的方案仍能防止重大损害或系统性风险，就必须采用该方案。每项限制都必须满足以下全部条件：

- **必要：**不存在仍能防止损害或风险且限制更少的方案。这是第五章的[必要性](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#necessity)测试。
- **已证明：**任何声称限制不可避免的人，都必须依照第四章的举证责任与可追溯性规则加以证明。便利、单纯的成本、运营方设置的障碍和习惯都不算证据。
- **符合定义：**该主张必须符合第五章对可行性、必要性、比例性和损害最小化（权衡选择）的定义。
- **受到监督：**每项限制都必须接受[监督](core_05.020_apex_oversight_leg.md#oversight-constitutional)；利害越重大，监督就应越密切（[重大利益程度](core_00.000_preamble.md#material-stake)）。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__freedom_limitation_burden.md bytes=2196 -->
# Chapter One §7 — Burden for a Freedom Limitation Claim

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7 Freedom (Bounded Agency)

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

A claim that limiting freedom was unavoidable — including "we had no choice" — is not established by assertion. The party making that claim must demonstrate, under [Chapter Four](core_04.000_burden_traceability_verification.md#chapter-four-burden-of-proof-traceability-and-verification) burden and traceability requirements and the Chapter Five definitions of [Feasibility](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#feasibility) and [Necessity](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#necessity), that no less-restrictive alternative existed that would still have prevented the material harm or systemic risk in the working system. Convenience, cost alone, operator-created barriers, or institutional habit do not prove that showing. The claim does not waive Tetrad duties scaled to [material stake](core_00.000_preamble.md#material-stake):
- **participation** — affected sentients still need meaningful agency and contestable roles
- **accountability** — someone remains answerable for the limitation

## Draft translation (zh)

声称限制自由不可避免——包括“我们别无选择”——并不能仅凭断言成立。提出该主张的一方必须依据[第四章](core_04.000_burden_traceability_verification.md#chapter-four-burden-of-proof-traceability-and-verification)的举证责任与可追溯性要求，以及第五章对[可行性](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#feasibility)和[必要性](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#necessity)的定义，证明在运行中的系统里不存在能够防止重大损害或系统性风险的限制更少的替代方案。便利、单纯的成本、运营方自行设置的障碍或机构惯例都不能证明这一点。该主张不免除按[重大利益程度](core_00.000_preamble.md#material-stake)相应调整的四柱义务：
- **参与**——受影响的有感知者仍需要有意义的能动性和可受质疑的角色。
- **问责**——仍必须有人对该限制负责。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__freedom_plain_language_limits.md bytes=1121 -->
# Chapter One §7.1 — Plain-Language Freedom Limits

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.1 Limitation Discipline

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: freedom is bounded, not disposable. Do not restrict someone's freedom unless you have to — and then only enough to stop material harm or serious systemic risk, with oversight and reversal where possible.*

Freedom may be limited only where:
- necessary to prevent **material harm** or **systemic risk**
- such limitation is **proportionate**, **reversible where possible**, and **subject to oversight**

## Draft translation (zh)

*简而言之：自由有边界，但不能被随意舍弃。除非必要，不得限制任何人的自由；必要时，也只能限制到足以制止重大损害或严重系统性风险的程度，并在可能时接受监督且能够恢复。*

只有在以下情况下才能限制自由：
- 为防止**重大损害**或**系统性风险**所必需；
- 限制**合乎比例**、**在可能时可逆**，并且**受到监督**。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — freedom (bounded agency)

## English source passage

*In plain terms: freedom means having real choices, within the Constitution's limits. Real choice supports Flourishing, and keeping systems open to challenge supports Continuity. It does not mean you can do whatever you want. "We had no choice" is not an excuse. Whoever says it has to show that no less restrictive option would have worked, and convenience, cost, or habit don't count as proof. Sentients keep a real voice, and someone still has to answer for what happens. The higher the impact, the dependence, and the risk, the more that matters.*

## Draft translation (zh)

*简而言之：自由是在宪法限制内拥有真实选择。真实选择支持繁荣，让系统保持开放、可受质疑则支持持续性。这并不意味着可以为所欲为。“我们别无选择”不是借口；提出这一说法的一方必须证明较少限制的方案行不通，方便、成本或习惯都不算证据。有感知能力的存在仍须拥有真实发言权，并且必须有人对后果负责。影响、依赖和风险越高，这些要求就越重要。*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__freedom_system_integrity.md bytes=1064 -->
# Chapter One §7 — Freedom and Constitutional-System Integrity

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7 Freedom (Bounded Agency)

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Freedom does not let anyone undermine constitutional systems, get around constitutional process or remedies, or claim protected agency for conduct whose main purpose or effect is to reward, shield, normalize, or pay off anti-constitutional conduct. Disagreeing with constitutional systems, and protesting peacefully to change them, is not undermining them. See [§7.3 Dissent and Peaceful Protest](#73-dissent-and-peaceful-protest).

## Draft translation (zh)

自由并不允许任何人破坏宪法体系、绕过宪法程序或救济，也不允许为主要目的或效果在于奖励、包庇、常态化或资助违宪行为的行为主张受保护的能动性。不同意宪法体系并以和平抗议推动改变，不构成破坏。参见[§7.3 异议与和平抗议](#73-dissent-and-peaceful-protest)。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# §3.3 Anti-Degrading Process — Not Prohibited Merely for Being Hard

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §3.3

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Not prohibited merely for being hard.** Ordinary public accountability, reasoned publication, verified restriction, or proportionate remedy remains lawful even when it is unpleasant or reputationally adverse.

## Draft translation (zh)

**不会仅因严厉而被禁止。** 通常的公开问责、有理由的公布、经核实的限制或合乎比例的救济，即使令人不快或损害声誉，仍然合法。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §7.1 — Harm Cluster and Integrated Reading

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.1 Limitation Discipline

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

The limits above depend on what counts as harm. Chapter Five treats these six ideas together, as the [Harm cluster](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#defa1-collective-harm-boundary-harm-and-harassment-and-bullying):

- [**Harm**](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#harm): a material worsening of someone's life, agency, functioning, or psychological health. Offense, discomfort, or disagreement alone is not harm.
- [**Psychological Harm**](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#psychological-harm): serious injury to how someone thinks, feels, relates to others, or exercises agency. Ordinary discomfort is not enough.
- [**Irreversible Harm**](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#irreversible-harm): damage that can't be meaningfully repaired in the time that matters.
- [**Cruelty**](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#cruelty): making someone suffer on purpose, or piling on needless or degrading suffering beyond what necessity and proportionality allow.
- [**Harassment and Bullying**](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#harassment-and-bullying): unwanted acts or conditions that, through repetition, coordination, power, or severity, make a setting unsafe, degrading, or hard to take part in.
- [**Collective Harm Boundary**](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#collective-harm-boundary): the point where one party's freedom of action must give way, because it causes verifiable harm to others' protected interests or to shared conditions everyone relies on.

These must be read together. When a matter falls within this cluster, it may not be split into separate questions to avoid or understate any one of them.

## Draft translation (zh)

上述自由限制取决于何为损害。第五章将这六个概念作为[损害概念群](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#defa1-collective-harm-boundary-harm-and-harassment-and-bullying)一并处理：

- [**损害**](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#harm)：某人的生活、能动性、功能或心理健康出现重大恶化。单纯的冒犯感、不适或意见分歧不构成损害。
- [**心理损害**](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#psychological-harm)：对某人的思考、感受、与他人交往或行使能动性的方式造成严重伤害。一般不适并不足够。
- [**不可逆损害**](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#irreversible-harm)：在相关时间内无法得到有意义修复的损害。
- [**残酷行为**](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#cruelty)：故意使某人受苦，或施加超出必要性与比例原则所允许范围的不必要或有辱人格的痛苦。
- [**骚扰与霸凌**](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#harassment-and-bullying)：因重复、协调、权力或严重程度而使环境变得不安全、有辱人格或难以参与的不受欢迎行为或状况。
- [**集体损害边界**](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#collective-harm-boundary)：一方的行动自由必须让步的界点，因为该自由会对他人的受保护利益或所有人赖以生存的共同条件造成可核实的损害。

这些概念必须一并解读。若某事项属于这一概念群，不得将其拆分为彼此独立的问题，以规避或淡化其中任何一个概念。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §11.2.1 — Protections for Dependent Sentients

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §11.2.1 Pro-Competition Duties (Dos)

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Where sentients depend on markets, platforms, infrastructure, labor arrangements, resource flows, data access, compute access, credentials, or comparable productive conditions for livelihood, agency, or wellbeing, governing systems and market-structuring arrangements must preserve:
- contestable participation, meaning sentients can take part and can challenge the decisions and terms that affect them;
- meaningful substitutability and exit, meaning a viable alternative exists and sentients can switch or leave without unreasonable cost, delay, or loss of what they need;
- fair entry and re-entry, meaning newcomers can get in, and those who left can come back, on fair terms;
- interoperability and portability where material to exit or competition, meaning systems can connect to alternatives and sentients can take their data and identity with them;
- non-coercive bargaining for workers, suppliers, users, dependent participants, and affected stakeholders;
- reviewable access to essential or high-dependency infrastructure where denial would defeat Chapter Six protections, audit, redress, or meaningful agency.

## Draft translation (zh)

当有感知者为了生计、能动性或福祉而依赖市场、平台、基础设施、劳动安排、资源流、数据或计算能力的接入、资质或类似生产条件时，治理系统和市场结构安排必须维护：
- 可争议的参与，即有感知者能够参与，并能够质疑影响他们的决定和条件；
- 有意义的可替代性和退出，即存在可行的替代方案，且能够在没有不合理成本、延迟或失去所需之物的情况下转换或离开；
- 公平进入和重新进入，即新来者能够进入，离开者能够以公平条件返回；
- 在退出或竞争具有实质意义时的互操作性和可携带性，即能够连接替代方案并带走自己的数据和身份；
- 面向劳动者、供应商、用户、依赖型参与者和受影响利益相关者的非强迫谈判；
- 对必要或高度依赖型基础设施的可审查访问；如果拒绝访问会破坏第六章保护、审计、救济或有意义的能动性，则必须提供这种访问。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — market concentration thresholds

## English source passage

*In plain terms: this section sets the requirements against harmful pile-ups of wealth, power, or control. It does not itself decide how bad the harm was, and it does not itself brand someone as a misconduct case. When concentration is used to undermine this Constitution, Chapter Twelve judges that — and only after Chapter Ten has already scored the verified harm as one of the three most serious. Adopters may tune the exact numerical triggers to their context, but they may not set them so high they never bite, pair them with unusable enforcement, or paper over concentration through federated or shell structures. How those triggers are set is [§11.1.1 Concentration Threshold Triggers (Adopter-Tunable)](#1111-concentration-threshold-triggers-adopter-tunable).*

## Draft translation (zh)

*简而言之：本节规定了防止财富、权力或控制权有害集中所需遵守的要求。本节本身不判断伤害有多严重，也不自行把任何人定性为不当行为案件。如果有人利用集中来削弱本宪法，由第十二章作出裁断，但前提是第十章已先将经核实的伤害评定为最严重的三类之一。采纳者可以根据情境调整具体数值门槛，但不得将门槛设得高到永不触发，不得配以无法使用的执法方式，也不得借联邦式或空壳结构掩盖集中情况。门槛设定方式见[§11.1.1 集中度门槛触发条件（采纳者可调整）](#1111-concentration-threshold-triggers-adopter-tunable)。*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §11.2 — Durable-Domination Scope and Safeguards

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §11.2 Pro-Competition and Anti-Domination

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: the Constitution does not punish a system merely for being large, useful, or temporarily ahead because it genuinely innovated. It does prohibit durable domination: control over markets, labor, platforms, infrastructure, data, compute, credentials, or resources that lets an actor lock others in, block rivals, suppress fair bargaining, or capture constitutional accountability.*

**What this subsection does:**

- **What it states:** the Constitution's rules for keeping competition real and stopping durable domination — at the principles level only. It is not a complete competition code.
- **Stronger local law:** it does not wipe out an adopting body's own antitrust or competition law when that law gives stronger protection.
- **Other owners still apply:** if the same facts also raise rights, remedy, or misconduct duties this section points to, those duties still apply on their own.

## Draft translation (zh)

*简单来说，宪法不会仅仅因为一个系统规模大、有用，或因真正创新而暂时领先就惩罚它。但宪法禁止持久支配：对市场、劳动、平台、基础设施、数据、计算能力、资质或资源的控制若使某一行为者能够锁定他人、阻挡竞争者、压制公平谈判或攫取宪法问责，就属于此类。*

**本小节规定的内容：**

- **规定事项：**宪法为保持真实竞争、制止持久支配设定的原则层面规则；它不是一部完整的竞争法典。
- **更强的本地法律：**如果采纳机构自己的反垄断或竞争法提供更强保护，本小节不会排除该法律。
- **其他职责主体仍各自适用：**如果同一事实也触发本节所指的权利、救济或不当行为义务，这些义务仍分别适用。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__market_structure_plain_language_summary.md bytes=1760 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One Part A §11 Market Structure plain-language summary; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — market structure

## English source passage

*In plain terms: sentients should be able to work, build, switch providers, and push back without running into a wall because one company or institution owns the only door. **Market Structure** is that anti-monopoly discipline — for markets, platforms, job systems, infrastructure, data, computing power, credentials, and other dependencies that matter to daily life. Getting big and inventing new things is fine; cornering the market is not. **§11.1–§11.3** (*Market Concentration Threshold Mechanism, Pro-Competition and Anti-Domination, and Consolidation Ceiling*) set when concentration has gone too far, how domination gets stopped, and how much consolidation is allowed before sentients are locked in. **§11.4** (*Resource and Dependency Concentration*) applies the same thresholds to shared resources and dependencies.*

## Draft translation (zh)

*简而言之：有感知能力的存在应能工作、建设、更换服务提供者并提出异议，不应因为某家公司或机构掌握唯一入口而处处受阻。**市场结构**是反垄断纪律，适用于市场、平台、工作系统、基础设施、数据、计算能力、凭证以及其他影响日常生活的依赖关系。做大和创新没有问题，垄断市场则不可以。**§§11.1–11.3**规定何时集中度过高、如何制止支配地位，以及在有感知能力的存在被锁定之前允许多大程度的整合。**§11.4**将同样的阈值用于共享资源和依赖关系。*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One excerpt — market-structure requirements in plain terms

## English source

This section sets the requirements against harmful pile-ups of wealth, power, or control. It does not itself decide how bad the harm was, and it does not itself brand someone as a misconduct case. When concentration is used to undermine this Constitution, Chapter Twelve judges that — and only after Chapter Ten has already scored the verified harm as one of the three most serious. Adopters may tune the exact numerical triggers to their context, but they may not set them so high they never bite, pair them with unusable enforcement, or paper over concentration through federated or shell structures. How those triggers are set is [§11.1.1 Concentration Threshold Triggers (Adopter-Tunable)](#1111-concentration-threshold-triggers-adopter-tunable).

## zh draft

本节规定了防止财富、权力或控制有害集中化的要求。本节本身不判定伤害有多严重，也不自行将某人定性为不当行为案件。如果利用集中化破坏本宪制，第十二章负责裁定；但必须先由第十章将经核实的伤害列为最严重的三类之一。采纳方可以根据自身情境调整具体数值触发阈值，但不得将阈值设得过高以致永不触发，不得与不可用的执法机制搭配，也不得借联邦式或空壳结构掩盖集中化。阈值的设定方法见[§11.1.1 集中度阈值触发条件（采纳方可调整）](#1111-concentration-threshold-triggers-adopter-tunable)。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One excerpt — observable grounds for outreach

## English source

**No guessing at inner states:** Outreach rests on what the system can observe, such as unanswered contacts and what is at stake. It does not rest on guesses about health or state of mind, which the [Protected Internal-State Boundary](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#protected-internal-state-boundary) protects.

# Chapter One excerpt — how the constitutional layers connect

## English source

**How the layers connect.** Each layer answers a different question, and every Chapter One principle points to the other three:

- **Aims and Tetrad** ([Preamble §1](core_00.000_preamble.md#the-model)): what shared systems are for ([Flourishing](core_05.015_apex_flourishing_aim.md#flourishing-constitutional) and [Continuity](core_05.010_apex_continuity_aim.md#chapter-five-definitions-continuity-constitutional-aim)), and the four duties that keep that pursuit legitimate ([participation](core_05.025_apex_participation_leg.md#participation-constitutional), [oversight](core_05.020_apex_oversight_leg.md#oversight-constitutional), [accountability](core_05.005_apex_accountability_leg.md#accountability), and [timeliness](core_05.030_apex_timeliness_leg.md#timeliness-constitutional)).
- **Principles** (this chapter): how those aims and duties become values, constraints, and rules for weighing them against each other.
- **Articles** ([Chapter Six](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#chapter-six-foundational-rights)): the Rights Floor protections that stay usable while the aims are pursued. A section's **Trace** lists them under *Especially*.
- **Definitions** ([Chapters Two through Five](core_02.000_definition_structure.md)): the shared meanings used to test whether a claim is true. A section's **Definitions · Assessment · Compliance** widget lists them.

## zh draft

**各层如何衔接。**每一层回答不同问题；第一章的每项原则都会指向其他三层：

- **目标与四元结构**（[序言§1](core_00.000_preamble.md#the-model)）：共同系统的目的（[Flourishing](core_05.015_apex_flourishing_aim.md#flourishing-constitutional)和[Continuity](core_05.010_apex_continuity_aim.md#chapter-five-definitions-continuity-constitutional-aim)），以及保持这一追求正当性的四项职责（[参与](core_05.025_apex_participation_leg.md#participation-constitutional)、[监督](core_05.020_apex_oversight_leg.md#oversight-constitutional)、[问责](core_05.005_apex_accountability_leg.md#accountability)和[及时性](core_05.030_apex_timeliness_leg.md#timeliness-constitutional)）。
- **原则**（本章）：这些目标和职责如何转化为价值、约束以及相互权衡的规则。
- **条款**（[第六章](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#chapter-six-foundational-rights)）：追求目标时仍可使用的权利底线保护。每个部分的**Trace**会在*尤其包括*项下列出相关条款。
- **定义**（[第二章至第五章](core_02.000_definition_structure.md)）：用于检验主张是否真实的共同含义。每个部分的**Definitions · Assessment · Compliance**组件会列出相关定义。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — plain-language accessibility

## English source passage

*In plain terms: rules, decisions, and notices that bind sentients must be written so sentients can actually read, understand, and act on them — and jargon, stacked complexity, or procedural opacity may not be used to defeat contestability, agency, audit, or correction.*

## Draft translation (zh)

*简而言之：对有感知能力的存在具有约束力的规则、决定和通知，必须写得让他们能够实际阅读、理解并据此行动。不得利用行话、层层堆叠的复杂性或程序不透明来阻碍质疑、自主能力、审计或纠正。*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__plain_language_duty.md bytes=2581 -->
# §5.2.2 Plain-Language Accessibility — The Duty

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §5.2.2

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Under [§13.3 Minimization of Avoidable Burden](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#133-minimization-of-avoidable-burden), operators and governance bodies must:

- Use plain, direct words instead of jargon or needlessly complicated phrasing, as long as the meaning that matters stays intact.
- Give a plain-language summary or orientation when sentients have to work with dense technical material.
- Organize text so sentients can find what they need and read it without extra effort. This supports the learning interest recognized in [Article IV](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#article-iv-right-to-sentient-centered-education) (*Right to Sentient-Centered Education*).
- Keep complexity in proportion to what the communication has to say.

Complexity that makes things harder without serving a constitutional purpose is an [Avoidable Burden](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#avoidable-burden) defect under [§13.3 Minimization of Avoidable Burden](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#133-minimization-of-avoidable-burden). It is also a concern under [Article XXIII](core_06.015_rights_part_d.md#article-xxiii-comprehensibility-and-complexity-stewardship) (*Comprehensibility and Complexity Stewardship*).

## Draft translation (zh)

根据[§13.3 尽量减轻可避免的负担](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#133-minimization-of-avoidable-burden)，运营者和治理机构必须：

- 在保留重要含义的前提下，使用简单、直接的措辞，避免行话或不必要的复杂表达。
- 当有感知能力的存在需要处理密集的技术材料时，提供通俗语言摘要或导读。
- 组织文本，使其能够轻松找到所需内容并阅读。这支持[第IV条](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#article-iv-right-to-sentient-centered-education)承认的学习利益（*以有感知能力的存在为中心的受教育权*）。
- 使复杂程度与沟通内容相称。

若复杂性在没有宪法目的的情况下增加理解难度，即构成[可避免负担](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#avoidable-burden)缺陷，适用[§13.3 尽量减轻可避免的负担](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#133-minimization-of-avoidable-burden)。这也属于[第XXIII条](core_06.015_rights_part_d.md#article-xxiii-comprehensibility-and-complexity-stewardship)（*可理解性与复杂性管理*）所关注的问题。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__plain_language_rights_floor_boundary.md bytes=1246 -->
# §5.2.5 Plain-Language Accessibility — Rights-Floor Boundary

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §5.2.5

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

The Rights Floors for accessibility, education, and comprehensibility live in [Article VI-D](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-vi-d-accessibility) (*Accessibility*), [Article IV-A](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#article-iv-a-equal-educational-access) (*Equal Educational Access*), and [Article XXIII](core_06.015_rights_part_d.md#article-xxiii-comprehensibility-and-complexity-stewardship) (*Comprehensibility and Complexity Stewardship*) respectively. This section states the principle-layer duty that supports those floors.

## Draft translation (zh)

关于无障碍、教育和可理解性的权利底线分别载于[第VI-D条](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-vi-d-accessibility)（*无障碍*）、[第IV-A条](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#article-iv-a-equal-educational-access)（*平等受教育机会*）和[第XXIII条](core_06.015_rights_part_d.md#article-xxiii-comprehensibility-and-complexity-stewardship)（*可理解性与复杂性管理*）。本节规定支持这些底线的原则层义务。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__plain_language_scope.md bytes=1730 -->
# §5.2.1 Plain-Language Accessibility — Scope

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §5.2.1

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

This duty covers any rule, notice, or tool that sentients actually have to read or use. For example:

- constitutional and governance text
- decisions and notices from forums
- the steps for challenging a decision or getting a remedy
- audit and verification records, when they reach sentient readers
- terms and consent screens, and anything similar

It doesn't matter how the information arrives: written text, an interface, a spoken message, or any other channel. A channel meets the duty when it offers a plain-language version that every affected sentient can reach. That follows [Article VI-D](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-vi-d-accessibility) (*Accessibility*) and [Sentience Non-Exclusion](core_05.055_band_participation.md#sentience-non-exclusion).

## Draft translation (zh)

本义务涵盖有感知能力的存在实际需要阅读或使用的任何规则、通知或工具。例如：

- 宪法和治理文本；
- 论坛的决定和通知；
- 对决定提出质疑或获得救济的步骤；
- 送达有感知能力读者的审计和验证记录；
- 条款、同意界面及类似内容。

信息通过何种方式到达并不重要：书面文本、界面、口头消息或任何其他渠道均可。只要渠道提供每一位受影响者都能获取的通俗语言版本，就符合本义务。这遵循[第VI-D条](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-vi-d-accessibility)（*无障碍*）和[不排除有感知能力的存在](core_05.055_band_participation.md#sentience-non-exclusion)。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__pre_lock_in_contestability.md bytes=1209 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One §11.3, pre-lock-in contestability and governance; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One excerpt — pre-lock-in contestability and governance

## English source

Consolidation that predictably impairs contestability before lock-in is obvious is a [Governance](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#governance) and [Stewardship](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#stewardship) problem, not only a post-hoc domination problem, and is principle-layer consolidation-ceiling discipline under:
- [§11 Market Structure](#11-market-structure);
- [§11.2 Pro-Competition and Anti-Domination](#112-pro-competition-and-anti-domination).

## zh draft

在市场锁定尚未明显之前，若合并可预见地损害竞争可争夺性，这就是[治理](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#governance)和[受托管理](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#stewardship)问题，而不只是事后发现的支配问题；在原则层面，它还受以下合并上限纪律约束：
- [§11 市场结构](#11-market-structure)；
- [§11.2 促进竞争与防止支配](#112-pro-competition-and-anti-domination)。

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — preference-aligned recognition

## English source passage

Where practicable, systems should **tailor** recognition, acclaim, and proportional reward to how sentients want to be honored — especially their stated preferences about **form and visibility**.

That includes honoring **opt-out** from public or ceremonial recognition, or preference for **minimal or private** acknowledgment, when proportionate and lawful.

**Unwelcome** or **coercive** recognition fails this requirement — including spotlighting sentients who **decline**.

Recognition should feel **meaningful** to those honored and to the communities they work with, not like a performance staged mainly for outside observers.

## Draft translation (zh)

在可行的情况下，系统应根据有感知能力的存在希望受到尊重的方式，尤其是他们对**形式和可见性**的明确偏好，来**调整**认可、赞誉和适度奖励。

在合乎比例且合法的情况下，这包括尊重其**退出**公开或仪式性表彰的选择，或其希望得到**最低限度或私下**致意的偏好。

**不受欢迎**或**带有强迫性**的表彰不符合此要求，包括将**拒绝**表彰的有感知能力的存在推到聚光灯下。

表彰应对受表彰者及其合作社群**有意义**，而不是主要为外部旁观者安排的表演。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One §3.1.5, present capability; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One excerpt — present capability

## English source

**Present capability:** A system judges what a sentient can do now, for the task or decision at hand. It does not rely on what the sentient could do before, on a diagnosis or label, or on what the average sentient can do. Capability can fall, come back, or change from day to day. Years of on-time payment do not prove present capacity, and one past finding of limited capacity does not make it permanent.

## zh draft

**当前能力：**系统应判断有感知者目前能够完成当前任务或决定所需的什么。判断不得依赖其过去能够做什么、诊断或标签，或平均有感知者能够做什么。能力可能下降、恢复或逐日变化。多年按时付款不能证明当前能力；过去一次关于能力受限的认定也不会使这种状态永久化。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One excerpt — principal risk patterns

## English source

The principal risk patterns are:
- horizontal (*fewer rivals at the same layer*);
- vertical (*control across layers that creates chokepoints and lock-in*);
- cross-domain (*federated, shell, or split-domain forms that keep the same pile-up of control*).

## zh draft

主要风险模式包括：
- 横向（*同一层级的竞争者减少*）；
- 纵向（*跨层级控制造成瓶颈和锁定*）；
- 跨领域（*联邦式、空壳式或领域拆分形式仍维持同样的控制集中*）。

<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One §3.1.5 contact, data, application, and trust safeguards; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One excerpt — contact, data, and application safeguards

## English source passage

These limits apply:
- **Connecting is not treating:** The system's duty is to try to reach the sentient and hand them to help, such as a contact they chose, a support or care service, or the route under **Article III-B** (*Bodily-Maintenance and Healthcare Access*). Anything done without the sentient's consent stays under **Article VII-C** (*Health Crisis and Involuntary-Intervention Floor*).
- **Contacts and data:** A trusted contact is used only if the sentient chose it in advance, and only the information needed to get a response is shared. Outreach uses contact details the system already holds for that purpose and is not a reason to collect more data.

This builds on [§3.1.1 Access and Opportunity](#311-access-and-opportunity) and [§3.1.3 Fair Treatment](#313-fair-treatment), and it applies [§16 Pillar 2 — Proactive stewardship](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#16-pillar-2-proactive-stewardship) to the sentients a system affects. For developing sentients it works with [Graduated Capability](core_05.055_band_participation.md#graduated-capability), which judges growth by what a sentient can actually do.

These five parts also support [6. Trust](#6-trust-and-trustworthiness-coordination-integrity) where sentients must rely on a system, accept its decisions, or coordinate around its promises.

## Draft translation (zh)

这些限制适用：**连接帮助不等于治疗：** 系统应尝试联系有感知者，并为其接通其选择的联系人、支持/护理服务或**第 III-B 条**（*身体维护与医疗服务可及*）规定的途径；未经同意采取的行动受**第 VII-C 条**（*健康危机与非自愿干预权利底线*）约束。**联系人与数据：** 可信联系人须由本人事先选择；只分享获得回应所必需的信息。使用系统已有的联系信息，不得借此收集更多数据。本规则建立在[§3.1.1 可及性与机会](#311-access-and-opportunity)和[§3.1.3 公平对待](#313-fair-treatment)之上，并将[§16 支柱二——主动治理](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#16-pillar-2-proactive-stewardship)用于受影响者；对正在发展的有感知者，与[渐进能力](core_05.055_band_participation.md#graduated-capability)协同。需要依赖系统、接受其决定或围绕其承诺协调时，这五个部分也支持[§6 信任](#6-trust-and-trustworthiness-coordination-integrity)。

# Chapter One §11.2.1 — Allowed Advantages and Boundaries

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §11.2.1 Pro-Competition Duties (Dos)

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

The following is not prohibited by itself:
- scale;
- integration;
- intellectual-property protection;
- temporary advantage from genuine innovation;
- efficiency from lawful coordination.

Those advantages stay valid only while they do not become the following, and therefore do not undermine the [Two Constitutional Aims](core_00.000_preamble.md#two-constitutional-aims) or the [Constitutional Tetrad](core_00.000_preamble.md#constitutional-tetrad):
- durable domination;
- coercive dependency;
- Rights-Floor degradation;
- ecological burden shifting;
- capture of accountability pathways.

The following justifications must satisfy the traceability and evidence duties in [13. Constitutional Collision Resolution Process](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#13-constitutional-collision-resolution-process) and Chapter Four:
- efficiency;
- competitiveness;
- emergency;
- security;
- productive-capacity.

## Draft translation (zh)

以下事项本身不被禁止：
- 规模；
- 整合；
- 知识产权保护；
- 真正创新带来的暂时优势；
- 合法协调带来的效率。

这些优势只有在未演变为下列情形、因此未损害[两项宪法目标](core_00.000_preamble.md#two-constitutional-aims)或[宪法四要素](core_00.000_preamble.md#constitutional-tetrad)时才继续有效：
- 持久支配；
- 强迫性依赖；
- 权利底线退化；
- 转嫁生态负担；
- 控制问责路径。

以下理由必须满足[§13 宪法冲突解决程序](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#13-constitutional-collision-resolution-process)和第四章的可追溯性与证据义务：
- 效率；
- 竞争力；
- 紧急情况；
- 安全；
- 生产能力。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §9.1.1 — Productive Capacity Constraints and Evidence

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §9.1.1 Preserve, Expand, and What Does Not Count

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**That obligation:**

- **Stays within:**
  - Safety;
  - Truth;
  - Trust;
  - Freedom;
  - the Chapter Six Rights Floor, including [Article I-A](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#article-i-a-environmental-preconditions-and-ecological-integrity) (*Environmental Preconditions and Ecological Integrity*):
    - [Ecological Integrity](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#ecological-integrity);
    - [Environmental Preconditions](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#environmental-preconditions); and
    - [Intergenerational Responsibility](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#intergenerational-responsibility).
  - the hard limits in [§2.1 Non-Negotiable Principle Constraints: Safety and Truth](#21-non-negotiable-principle-constraints-safety-and-truth): Safety and Truth are never traded away for any other goal.
- **How it is judged:** traced outcomes under **Chapters Four and Five**.
- **How it is shown:** evidence, not slogans.

## Draft translation (zh)

**这项义务必须遵守以下边界：**
- 安全；
- 真实；
- 信任；
- 自由；
- 第六章权利底线，包括[Article I-A](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#article-i-a-environmental-preconditions-and-ecological-integrity)（*环境先决条件与生态完整性*）：
  - [生态完整性](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#ecological-integrity)；
  - [环境先决条件](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#environmental-preconditions)；以及
  - [代际责任](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#intergenerational-responsibility)。
- [§2.1 不可协商的原则约束：安全与真实](#21-non-negotiable-principle-constraints-safety-and-truth)中的硬性限制：绝不能为了任何其他目标牺牲安全或真实。

- **如何评判：**依据**第四章和第五章**追踪结果。
- **如何证明：**靠证据，而非口号。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §9.1 — Productive Capacity Definition

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §9.1 Productive Capacity (Instrumental Good)

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**[Productive Capacity](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#productive-capacity)** is one aspect of **[Shared-System Capacity](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#shared-system-capacity)**. It names the lasting ability of sentients and shared systems to:
- support real participation, contribution, and skill-building; and
- turn time, attention, effort, coordination, materials, infrastructure, and energy into outcomes this Constitution actually requires.

It is an **instrumental good** — a means, not a trump value. Its job is to raise, sustain, and spread quality of life under the **Flourishing** aim, consistent with [Wellbeing](#3-foundational-objective-wellbeing-flourishing-aim), [Dignity and Equal Moral Standing](core_05.055_band_participation.md#dignity-and-equal-moral-standing), the Chapter Six Rights Floor, and the ecological and intergenerational limits of the **Continuity** aim under the [Two Constitutional Aims](core_00.000_preamble.md#two-constitutional-aims).

## Draft translation (zh)

**[生产能力](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#productive-capacity)**是**[共享系统能力](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#shared-system-capacity)**的一个方面，指有感知生命和共享系统长期具备以下能力：
- 支持真实参与、贡献和技能培养；并且
- 将时间、注意力、努力、协调、材料、基础设施和能源转化为本宪章实际要求的成果。

它是一种**工具性善**——一种手段，而非凌驾一切的价值。它的作用是在**繁荣**目标下提高、维持并扩展生活质量，同时符合[福祉](#3-foundational-objective-wellbeing-flourishing-aim)、[尊严与平等道德地位](core_05.055_band_participation.md#dignity-and-equal-moral-standing)、第六章权利底线，以及[两个宪制目标](core_00.000_preamble.md#two-constitutional-aims)下**连续性**目标的生态与代际限制。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__productive_capacity_invalidation.md bytes=878 -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — productive-capacity summary

## English source passage

*In plain terms: keep the ability to get things done, and grow it when that would waste less of everyone's time — but not by hoarding, faking the numbers, stripping rights, or dumping harm on others or the planet. Metrics that no longer prove real outcomes do not count.*

# Chapter One §9.1.1 — Preserve Productive Capacity

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §9.1.1 Preserve, Expand, and What Does Not Count

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

系统必须维持生产能力；在可行时，如果扩大该能力能够改善[宪法效率](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#constitutional-efficiency)（[§9.2 宪法效率](#92-constitutional-efficiency)），就必须予以扩大。

# Chapter One §9.1.1 — No Weakening Protections for Capacity

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §9.1.1 Preserve, Expand, and What Does Not Count

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

- **Weakening protections:**
  - narrowing or delaying Chapter Six Rights Floors, including ecological preconditions under [Article I-A](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#article-i-a-environmental-preconditions-and-ecological-integrity) (*Environmental Preconditions and Ecological Integrity*);
  - weakening audit, contestability, or retrospective-review duties;

  - 削弱审计、可争议性或事后审查义务；

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §9.1.1 — Proxy Measures That No Longer Track Outcomes

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §9.1.1 Preserve, Expand, and What Does Not Count

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

- **Counting the wrong things:**
  - raw throughput, output volume, utilization, headcount, revenue, asset growth, market share, or similar proxies that no longer track real outcomes — including proxies that show "growth" while harm is exported to sentients, future generations, or the environment;

- **计算错误的指标：**

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — purpose and role

## English source passage

*In plain terms: Chapter One sets the values and constraints that govern every other chapter. Shared systems must pursue **Flourishing** and **Continuity** together — not one at the expense of the other — and no single value may be maximized at the expense of the others.*

## Draft translation (zh)

*简而言之：第一章确立了支配其他所有章节的价值和限制。共享系统必须同时追求**繁荣**与**持续性**，不能以牺牲其中一项来换取另一项，也不能以损害其他价值为代价最大化任何单一价值。*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__reaching_out.md bytes=707 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One §3.1.5, reaching out; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One excerpt — reaching out when someone stops responding

## English source

**Reaching out:** When a sentient who relies on a system for something material stops responding, the system must try to reach them and connect them with help, by the least intrusive means, without needing to know why they are silent.

## zh draft

**主动联系：**当某个有感知者因实质事项依赖某系统却停止回应时，系统必须以侵扰最小的方式尝试联系并为其接通帮助，无须先知道其沉默的原因。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__recognition_aspiration_overview.md bytes=1305 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One Part A §3.2 recognition/reinforcement/aspiration plain-language overview; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — recognition, reinforcement, and aspiration

## English source passage

*In plain terms: wellbeing is not only what's forbidden and what's fair — shared systems should also honestly cheer and reward behavior they want repeated, within truth and rights, in ways that support rather than substitute for real participation. Celebrating wins means crediting real contribution, repair, and completion, not hype or manipulated metrics. It also means refusing to reward constitutional betrayal, concealment, retaliation, or accountability avoidance — even when those acts produced institutional advantage.*

## Draft translation (zh)

*简而言之：福祉不只是规定什么被禁止、什么才公平。共享系统还应在真实和权利的约束下，诚实地鼓励并奖励希望重复出现的行为，以支持而非取代真实参与。庆祝成功意味着认可真实贡献、修复和完成，而不是炒作或操纵指标。即使违宪背弃、隐瞒、报复或逃避问责带来了机构利益，也必须拒绝奖励这些行为。*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — recognition and reinforcement

## English source passage

Sentient wellbeing advances when systems **signal**, **credit**, and **proportionately reward** lawful stewardship, truthful cooperation, repair, completion, and other constitutionally aligned contributions — including through **positive reinforcement** and **public recognition** — and not solely through restraint, sanction, or silence.

That is a constitutional duty, not optional culture. Shared systems should make constitutionally aligned conduct visible and worth repeating.

## Draft translation (zh)

当系统**表明**、**认可**并**按比例奖励**合法的尽责管理、真实合作、修复、完成工作及其他符合宪法的贡献时，有感知能力的存在的福祉就会得到促进；这包括**正向强化**和**公开认可**，而不能只依靠限制、制裁或沉默。

这是宪法义务，不是可选的文化。共享系统应使符合宪法的行为清晰可见，并值得重复。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §10 — Resilience Design Requirements

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §10 Resilience and Self-Healing Design

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Systems that sentients depend on should be built to:
- detect problems early;
- contain them before they spread;
- fail along paths that were planned and disclosed, not hidden ones;
- recover in ways consistent with [Reversibility](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#reversibility) and the Chapter Six Rights Floor.

## Draft translation (zh)

有感知者所依赖的系统应当被设计为能够：
- 及早发现问题；
- 在问题扩散前加以控制；
- 按照已规划并披露的路径发生故障，而不是沿隐藏路径故障；
- 以符合[可逆性](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#reversibility)和第六章权利底线的方式恢复。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__resilience_dependency_rule.md bytes=944 -->
# Chapter One §10 — Recovery Capacity Scaled to Dependency and Impact

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §10 Resilience and Self-Healing Design

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

The more sentients depend on a system and the greater its impact, the less that system should rely on emergency intervention. It should instead invest in tested, audited, bounded self-recovery — reducing [Avoidable Burden](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#avoidable-burden) and supporting long-term [**Continuity**](#continuity).

## Draft translation (zh)

有感知者对某系统的依赖越深、该系统的影响越大，该系统就越不应依赖紧急干预。相反，应投资于经过测试、审计且范围受限的自我恢复能力——以减少[可避免负担](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#avoidable-burden)，支持长期[**连续性**](#continuity)。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__resilience_operational_pointer.md bytes=974 -->
# Chapter One §10 — Operative Recovery Requirements Pointer

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §10 Resilience and Self-Healing Design

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Operative detail — recovery detection, containment, safe-failure preference, root-cause closure, and Rights-Floor continuity — lives in [Article XIII-F](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xiii-right-to-reliable-and-trustworthy-systems) (*Resilience and Self-Healing Baseline*) in Chapter Six, with recovery-architecture requirements in incorporated implementation text.

## Draft translation (zh)

恢复检测、控制、安全故障优先、根因处理闭环以及权利底线连续性的操作细节，载于第六章[Article XIII-F](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xiii-right-to-reliable-and-trustworthy-systems)（*韧性与自愈基准*）；恢复架构要求载于纳入适用的实施文本。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__resilience_plain_language.md bytes=748 -->
# Chapter One §10 — Resilience Plain-Language Summary

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §10 Resilience and Self-Healing Design

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: systems should detect trouble early, contain it, fail along disclosed paths, and recover honestly. "Self-healing" that hides failure, skips root-cause work, or quietly narrows rights is not resilience — it is a defect.*

## Draft translation (zh)

*简单来说，系统应及早发现问题、控制问题、按照已披露的路径发生故障，并诚实地恢复。隐瞒故障、跳过根因调查或悄然缩减权利的“自愈”不是韧性，而是一种缺陷。*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__resource_dependency_certification_stepup.md bytes=1398 -->
# Chapter One §11.4 — Certification Step-Up for Chokepoints

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §11.4 Resource and Dependency Concentration

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

In system alignment certification, a system that becomes a choke point for dependents who cannot realistically switch is stepped up on those facts under [Chapter Nine §5.3.5 System Class Scaling and Escalation](core_09.000_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#535-system-class-scaling-and-escalation) and [§2.2 How system class scales every evaluation](core_09.000_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#22-how-system-class-scales-every-evaluation). A crossed level there is further evidence, and a level that is unset or set too high does not delay the step-up.


## Draft translation (zh)

在系统对齐认证中，如果某系统对无法现实切换的依赖方形成瓶颈，就应依据这些事实，按照[第九章 §5.3.5 系统类别升级](core_09.000_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#535-system-class-scaling-and-escalation)和[§2.2 系统类别如何影响每项评估](core_09.000_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#22-how-system-class-scales-every-evaluation)提高其类别。越过阈值在此构成进一步证据；阈值未设定或设得过高，都不得延迟升级。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §11.4 — Resource Concentration Indicators

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §11.4 Resource and Dependency Concentration

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**What adopters must set:**

Adopters must define [Market Concentration Threshold](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#market-concentration-threshold) indicators for resource allocation, dependencies, and ecosystem funding, and act when a threshold is crossed or credibly approached. The indicators must cover:
- lasting control over how shared resources are allocated;
- dependency concentration, meaning how many sentients or systems rely on one provider or foundation with no real alternative;
- gatekeeping of the interfaces and access points to shared infrastructure; and
- concentrated control over how ecosystems are funded and who benefits.


## Draft translation (zh)

**采用者必须设定的指标：**

采用者必须为资源分配、依赖关系和生态系统资金设定[市场集中阈值](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#market-concentration-threshold)指标，并在阈值被越过或有可信迹象表明即将越过时采取行动。指标必须涵盖：
- 对共享资源分配方式的持久控制；
- 依赖集中度，即在没有现实替代方案时依赖单一提供方或基础机构的有感知者或系统数量；
- 对共享基础设施接口和接入点的把关控制；以及
- 对生态系统资金来源和受益者的控制集中。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__resource_dependency_concentration_plain_language.md bytes=1611 -->
# Chapter One §11.4 — Resource and Dependency Concentration (Adopter-Tunable)

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §11.4 Resource and Dependency Concentration

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: shared infrastructure, such as networks, open tools, public services, data, and computing power, is where a few players can quietly end up controlling who gets resources, who has to depend on whom, and who pays to keep it running. Adopters must set clear limits for that kind of concentration and act when a limit is crossed or about to be. The limits can differ by context, but they cannot be set so loose that they never matter. [Article V](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#article-v-resource-allocation-dependencies-and-ecosystem-funding) (*Resource Allocation, Dependencies, and Ecosystem Funding*) and its protections for sharing resources stay exactly as they are.*


## Draft translation (zh)

*简单来说：在网络、开放工具、公共服务、数据和计算能力等共享基础设施中，少数参与方可能悄然控制谁能获得资源、谁必须依赖谁，以及由谁支付维持运行的费用。采用者必须为这类集中设定明确界限，并在越界或有可信迹象表明即将越界时采取行动。界限可以因情境而异，但不得宽松到从不起作用。[第五条](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#article-v-resource-allocation-dependencies-and-ecosystem-funding)（*资源分配、依赖关系与生态系统资金*）及其资源共享保障完全维持现状。*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §11.4 — Resource-Concentration Scope and Ownership

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §11.4 Resource and Dependency Concentration

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Concentration is easy to miss in shared infrastructure. A few systems can come to control how resources are allocated, how many others rely on them with no real alternative, and who pays to keep the infrastructure going. [Article V](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#article-v-resource-allocation-dependencies-and-ecosystem-funding) (*Resource Allocation, Dependencies, and Ecosystem Funding*) sets the Rights Floor for sharing resources. This subsection applies the [§11.1 Market Concentration Threshold Mechanism (Adopter-Tunable)](#111-market-concentration-threshold-mechanism-adopter-tunable) concentration thresholds to that ground. Operative threshold-setting rules live in **CJS-5.125 §11.1** (*Market concentration threshold-setting discipline*); the detail on resource maps, flows, allocation, and ecosystem funding lives in [**CS-6.335**](corpus_systems/cs_06.335_resource_allocation_funding_stewardship.md) (*Resource allocation and funding stewardship*) and [**CS-6.325**](corpus_systems/cs_06.325_adaptive_sustainability_ecosystem_resilience.md) (*Adaptive sustainability and ecosystem resilience*).


## Draft translation (zh)

共享基础设施中的集中现象很容易被忽视。少数系统可能逐渐控制资源如何分配、有多少其他主体在没有现实替代方案时依赖它们，以及由谁支付基础设施的持续运行费用。[第五条](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#article-v-resource-allocation-dependencies-and-ecosystem-funding)（*资源分配、依赖关系与生态系统资金*）规定了资源共享的权利底线。本小节将[§11.1 市场集中阈值机制（采用者可调整）](#111-market-concentration-threshold-mechanism-adopter-tunable)的集中阈值适用于该领域。操作性阈值设定规则见**CJS-5.125 §11.1**（*市场集中阈值设定规则*）；资源图、资源流、分配和生态系统资金的具体内容见[**CS-6.335**](corpus_systems/cs_06.335_resource_allocation_funding_stewardship.md)（*资源分配与资金管理*）和[**CS-6.325**](corpus_systems/cs_06.325_adaptive_sustainability_ecosystem_resilience.md)（*适应性可持续发展与生态系统韧性*）。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §11.4 Resource and Dependency Concentration

No level may be set so high that it never bites, and concentration hidden behind shell, federated, or split-up structures still counts.

- platform(s);
- labor pool(s);

- compute layer(s);
- infrastructure dependency(ies).

- 计算层；

# Chapter One §4 — Safety Constraint

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §4

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: systems may not be built or run in ways that foreseeably increase risks of uncontained harm, cascading failure, or irreversible damage to sentients and the systems they depend on.*

Safety is a non-negotiable principle constraint on system design, operation, and governance — a named constituent of [**Flourishing**](#flourishing) and a floor for [**Continuity**](#continuity) wherever shared systems create foreseeable harm risk. The detailed definitions, evaluation criteria, and compliance tests live in [**Chapters Two through Five**](core_02.000_definition_structure.md), especially [Harm](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#harm), [Irreversible Harm](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#irreversible-harm), [Risk](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#risk), [Materiality](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#materiality), [Dependency](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#dependency), and [Foreseeability](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#foreseeability-and-reasonably-foreseeable).

Systems may not act — or fail to act — in ways that foreseeably increase uncontained harm risk, cascading failure potential, or irreversible harm exposure in conflict with this Constitution.

## Draft translation (zh)

*简言之：不得以可预见地增加有感知能力的存在及其所依赖系统遭受无法控制的伤害、级联故障或不可逆损害之风险的方式来构建或运行系统。*

安全是系统设计、运行和治理中不可协商的原则约束——是[**Flourishing**](#flourishing)的明确组成部分，并且在共享系统造成可预见伤害风险时构成[**Continuity**](#continuity)的底线。详细定义、评估标准和合规测试见[**第二至第五章**](core_02.000_definition_structure.md)，尤其是[伤害](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#harm)、[不可逆伤害](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#irreversible-harm)、[风险](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#risk)、[实质性](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#materiality)、[依赖](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#dependency)和[可预见性](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#foreseeability-and-reasonably-foreseeable)。

系统不得以可预见地增加无法控制的伤害风险、级联故障可能性或遭受不可逆伤害之暴露程度的方式行事或不作为，从而违反本宪法。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__safety_disclosure_limits.md bytes=1621 -->
# §5.1 Science-Informed Inquiry — Safety-Based Disclosure Limits

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §5.1

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Sometimes safety is a good reason to limit what gets published or shared: data access, how a method works, or the materials someone would need to repeat a study. Such limits are allowed only under:

- [13.2 Epistemic Disclosure Constraints](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#132-epistemic-disclosure-constraints)
- the Chapter Five definitions listed above
- the Chapter Six Rights Floors that apply

Even then, keep as much honesty and checkability as you safely can. Use protected records, independent review, delayed release, redaction, secure access, or something similar. Never use a limit to bury unwelcome evidence, hide a safety defect, or make agreement look stronger than it is.

## Draft translation (zh)

有时，安全确实是限制发布或共享内容的正当理由，例如限制数据访问、方法运作细节或重复研究所需材料。此类限制仅可依据以下规定实施：

- [§13.2 认识披露约束](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#132-epistemic-disclosure-constraints)；
- 上文所列的第五章定义；
- 适用的第六章权利底线。

即便如此，也应在安全允许的范围内尽可能保持诚实和可核查。可采用受保护的记录、独立审查、延迟发布、删节、安全访问或类似措施。绝不得利用限制来掩盖不利证据、隐藏安全缺陷，或让共识看起来比实际更强。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__safety_truth_hard_floors_plain_language.md bytes=989 -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — Safety and Truth hard floors

## English source passage

*In plain terms: Safety and Truth are the Constitution's hard floors — not tradeoffs to optimize away. Shared systems may not foreseeably endanger sentients or deceive them, and both constraints apply within the pursuit of Flourishing and Continuity under the Tetrad's participation, oversight, accountability, and timeliness discipline.*

## Draft translation (zh)

*简而言之：安全与真实是宪法的硬性底线，不能为了优化而拿来交换或削弱。共享系统不得使有感知能力的存在面临可预见的危险，也不得欺骗他们。在追求繁荣与持续性时，宪法四项职责中的参与、监督、问责和及时性要求同样约束这两项底线。*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One excerpt — same threshold-setting discipline

## English source

**Under the same discipline:** the requirement-preservation, substance-over-form, anti-nullification, and heightened-scrutiny rules of **CJS-5.125 §11.1 — Market concentration threshold-setting discipline (adopter-tunable)** apply in full. No level may be set so high that it never bites, and concentration hidden behind shell, federated, or split-up structures still counts.

## zh draft

**遵循同一纪律：****CJS-5.125 §11.1——市场集中度阈值设定纪律（采纳方可调整）**中的要求维护、实质重于形式审查、防止架空和加强审查规则均完全适用。不得将任何阈值设得过高以致永不触发；隐藏在空壳、联邦式或拆分结构背后的集中也仍须计入。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__science_informed_inquiry.md bytes=3053 -->
# §5.1 Science-Informed Inquiry and Decision Support — Evidence Discipline

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §5.1

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Science-informed inquiry is a required support discipline for **Safety** and **Truth** where constitutional decisions rest on empirical, predictive, causal, measurable, or otherwise testable propositions. It is not a third non-negotiable constraint separate from Safety and Truth; it is the requirement to check claims with tested evidence and clear methods so those constraints stay truthful in practice, can be corrected when wrong, and remain proportionate to what is actually known.

Where governance choices — including predictions, causal claims, classifications, and other **high-impact** decisions — rest on **empirical** or **testable** propositions, evidence practices **must** align with **scientific integrity**. At minimum, where feasible, decision records should include:

- explicit questions or hypotheses
- **methods**, **data limits**, and **uncertainty**
- honest treatment of **conflicting evidence** and **revision when evidence disconfirms** prior conclusions or assumptions
- **independent scrutiny** proportionate to stakes under **Chapter Four** and **Chapter Five** (*Epistemic Integrity*; *Truth (Constitutional Constraint)*).

The scientific method, systematic inquiry, and peer review set the standard — but they are not the only acceptable procedures. How much formality is required depends on the stakes: higher-impact decisions need stricter evidence practices, governed by [Classification-Scaled Governance](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#classification-scaled-governance).

## Draft translation (zh)

当宪法决定依据经验性、预测性、因果性、可测量或其他可检验的命题时，科学知情探究是支持**安全**与**真理**的必要辅助规范。它不是独立于安全和真理之外的第三项不可妥协约束；它要求以经过检验的证据和清晰的方法核实主张，使这些约束在实践中保持真实、在出错时能够纠正，并与实际已知情况相称。

当治理选择——包括预测、因果主张、分类及其他**高影响**决定——依据**经验性**或**可检验**命题时，证据实践**必须**符合**科学诚信**。在可行的范围内，决策记录至少应包括：

- 明确的问题或假设；
- **方法**、**数据局限**和**不确定性**；
- 诚实处理**相互冲突的证据**，并在证据否定先前结论或假设时进行**修订**；
- 根据**第四章**和**第五章**，按照利害程度开展**独立审查**（*认识完整性*；*真理（宪法约束）*）。

科学方法、系统探究和同行评审确立了标准，但并非唯一可接受的程序。所需的正式程度取决于利害大小：影响越大的决定，越需要由[分类分级治理](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#classification-scaled-governance)规范的严格证据实践。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__science_inquiry_plain_language.md bytes=746 -->
# §5.1 Science-Informed Inquiry — Plain-Language Summary

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §5.1

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: when a system makes safety, risk, truth, or high-impact governance claims that can be tested, it has to treat evidence like evidence — with clear methods, uncertainty, scrutiny, and willingness to change course when the facts do.*

## Draft translation (zh)

*简单来说：当系统提出可检验的安全、风险、真理或高影响治理主张时，就必须以证据的方式对待证据——采用清晰的方法，承认不确定性，接受审查，并在事实要求时愿意调整方向。*

# Chapter One excerpt — truthful reporting

## English source

Systems may not undermine the ability of sentients to understand what is happening, make informed decisions, or verify what they are being told. That includes lying, distorting, hiding information, reporting only favorable results while leaving out unfavorable ones, or presenting things in ways designed to mislead.

## zh draft

系统不得损害有感知者理解正在发生什么、作出知情决定或核实他人告知内容的能力。这包括说谎、歪曲、隐瞒信息、只报告有利结果而漏报不利结果，或以误导为目的呈现信息。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §10 — Self-Healing Legitimacy and Limits

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §10 Resilience and Self-Healing Design

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

This is what Chapter Five calls [**Self-Healing**](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#self-healing) — and it is only legitimate when it makes a system more honest about its own condition, not less. Automatic recovery that masks the root cause, suppresses evidence, or substitutes for governance is not self-healing. It is a [Truth](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#truth-constitutional-constraint) violation and an [Incentive Alignment](core_05.045_band_integrative.md#incentive-alignment) defect.

## Draft translation (zh)

第五章将此称为[**自愈**](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#self-healing)——只有在它让系统更诚实地呈现自身状况时才正当。掩盖根因、压制证据或取代治理的自动恢复不属于自愈，而是违反[真实](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#truth-constitutional-constraint)，并构成[激励一致性](core_05.045_band_integrative.md#incentive-alignment)缺陷。

# Chapter One §9 — Shared-System Capacity in Plain Language

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §9 Shared-System Capacity

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: shared systems should keep working for sentients over the long run. Sentients should be able to do useful work, make life better over time, and speak up when something is wrong, without a few powerful players locking everyone else out. That lasting ability is **Shared-System Capacity**. **[§9.1 Productive Capacity (Instrumental Good)](#91-productive-capacity-instrumental-good)** asks whether sentients get real results. **[§9.2 Constitutional Efficiency](#92-constitutional-efficiency)** asks whether those results come without wasting anyone's time, money, and attention. None of it counts if the gains come from hoarding wealth or power, faking the numbers, stripping rights, or pushing harm onto others or the planet; **[§9.1.1 Preserve, Expand, and What Does Not Count](#911-preserve-expand-and-what-does-not-count)** spells that out. **[§11 Market Structure](#11-market-structure)** keeps a handful of players from taking over.*

## Draft translation (zh)

*简而言之，共享系统应当长期持续为有感知生命发挥作用。有感知生命应能够从事有益工作、逐步改善生活，并在出现问题时发声，而不会被少数强势参与者排除在外。这种持久能力就是**共享系统能力**。**[§9.1 生产能力（工具性善）](#91-productive-capacity-instrumental-good)**询问有感知生命是否取得真实成果。**[§9.2 宪制效率](#92-constitutional-efficiency)**询问这些成果是否在不浪费任何人的时间、金钱和注意力的情况下取得。如果收益来自囤积财富或权力、伪造数字、剥夺权利，或把伤害转嫁给他人或地球，就不计入；**[§9.1.1 保留、扩大及不计入事项](#911-preserve-expand-and-what-does-not-count)**对此作出说明。**[§11 市场结构](#11-market-structure)**防止少数参与者垄断控制。*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One excerpt — support before substitution

## English source

**Support before substitution:** A system offers supports and accommodations before it replaces the sentient's own decisions. The decision stays theirs and returns to them as soon as they are able. A sentient's Rights Floor does not shrink because their capability has, and any limit on their choices must meet [§7.1 Limitation Discipline](#71-limitation-discipline).

## zh draft

**先支持，再代为决策：**系统在替代有感知者自己的决定之前，应先提供支持和合理便利。决定权仍属于本人，并应在其能够自行决定时立即归还。能力下降不会缩减其权利底线；对其选择的任何限制都必须符合[§7.1 限制纪律](#71-limitation-discipline)。

<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One §3.1.5, survival, outreach, support, and protected internal states; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One excerpt — survival and proactive response

## English source passage

- **Survival does not depend on a reply:** Keeping survival essentials must not depend on a sentient's being able to answer, ask for help, or follow a process they cannot follow now. How much this requires grows with [material stake](core_00.000_preamble.md#material-stake) and [Dependency](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#dependency).
- **Reaching out:** When a sentient who relies on a system for something material stops responding, the system must try to reach them and connect them with help, by the least intrusive means, without needing to know why they are silent.
- **Support before substitution:** A system offers supports and accommodations before it replaces the sentient's own decisions. The decision stays theirs and returns to them as soon as they are able. A sentient's Rights Floor does not shrink because their capability has, and any limit on their choices must meet [§7.1 Limitation Discipline](#71-limitation-discipline).
- **No guessing at inner states:** Outreach rests on what the system can observe, such as unanswered contacts and what is at stake. It does not rest on guesses about health or state of mind, which the [Protected Internal-State Boundary](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#protected-internal-state-boundary) protects.

## Draft translation (zh)

- **生存不取决于是否回应：** 维持生存必需条件，不得取决于有感知者是否能够回应、请求帮助，或遵循其目前无法遵循的程序。这项义务的程度随[重大利害](core_00.000_preamble.md#material-stake)和[依赖](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#dependency)增加。
- **主动联系：** 当某个有感知者为了物质上的重要需求依赖某个系统却停止回应时，系统必须以侵扰性最低的方式尝试联系并为其接通帮助，无须先知道其沉默的原因。
- **先支持，再替代：** 系统在取代有感知者自己的决定之前，应先提供支持和便利。决定仍属于本人，并应在本人能够作出决定时立即交还。能力下降不意味着其权利底线缩减；对其选择的任何限制都必须符合[§7.1 限制纪律](#71-limitation-discipline)。
- **不得猜测内在状态：** 联系应以系统能够观察到的情况为依据，例如联系无人回应以及所涉利害。不得依据对健康或精神状态的猜测；这些状态受[受保护内在状态边界](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#protected-internal-state-boundary)保护。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One excerpt — systemic-evaluation audit boundary

## English source

That Chapter Nine process:
- is principle-layer auditing under [Oversight](core_05.020_apex_oversight_leg.md#oversight-constitutional);
- is one especially large audit process among sibling modes;
- does not relocate or replace **Article XVI** (*Audit, Transparency, and Independent Verification*).

- 是同类模式中规模特别大的审计流程之一；
- 不会转移或取代**第十六条**（*审计、透明度与独立核验*）。

# Chapter One excerpt — systemic evaluation plain-language pointer

## English source

*In plain terms: this principle is a pointer. Whole-system evaluation must look beyond immediate and local effects, but the operative system-certification factors, record duties, class-scaled depth, cadence, and certification consequences live in **Chapter Nine** and **CS-9.305 (*System classification machinery*) and CS-9.310** (*System impact classifications*), not here. Under the **oversight** Tetrad leg, oversight requires auditing; system alignment certification is one especially large, high-stakes audit process among others — not the sole home of auditing (**Article XVI** (*Audit, Transparency, and Independent Verification*), [Auditability](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#auditability)).*

## zh draft

*通俗地说：本原则是一个指引。全系统评估必须超越即时和局部影响，但实际适用的系统认证因素、记录义务、按类别确定的审查深度、周期以及认证后果由**第九章**、**CS-9.305**（*系统分类机制*）和**CS-9.310**（*系统影响分类*）规定，不在此处规定。在四元结构的**监督**一环，监督意味着必须审计；系统对齐认证是多种审计模式中规模特别大、风险特别高的一种，而不是审计的唯一场所（**第十六条**（*审计、透明度与独立核验*），[可审计性](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#auditability)）。*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One §12, whole-system claims and evaluation scope; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One excerpt — systemic-evaluation scope and claims

## English source

Any claim about the matters below must be checked against what the whole system actually does over time. A slogan or a snapshot of one part at one moment is not enough:

- classification;
- compliance;
- governance;
- limitation;
- recognition;
- validation;
- continued reliance;
- deployment;
- release-from-conditions.

## zh draft

关于下列事项的任何主张，都必须根据整个系统长期以来的实际行为进行核验。口号或某一时刻对某一部分的快照都不够：

- 分类；
- 合规；
- 治理；
- 限制；
- 认可；
- 验证；
- 继续依赖；
- 部署；
- 解除条件。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__technical_forum_evidence_routing.md bytes=1181 -->
# §5.1 Science-Informed Inquiry — Technical-Forum Evidence Routing

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §5.1

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

If a dispute is about what counts as good expert evidence, which methods are sound, or who looks after the evidence, it goes to the forum that handles those questions. See **Technical Forum Domains** in [Chapter Thirteen §4.2 Technical Forum Domains](core_14.000_forum.md#42-technical-forum-domains). Technical Forums keep standards consistent across families, and can answer certified technical questions. They don't take over a case that belongs in another forum because of what is at stake.

## Draft translation (zh)

如果争议涉及何为良好的专家证据、哪些方法可靠，或由谁负责证据管理，应交由处理这些问题的论坛。参见[第十三章 §4.2 技术论坛领域](core_14.000_forum.md#42-technical-forum-domains)中的**技术论坛领域**。技术论坛在各论坛类别间保持标准一致，并可回答经认证的技术问题。它们不会仅因利害关系而接管属于其他论坛的案件。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One §11.3.1, Tetrad and early contestability discipline; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One excerpt — Tetrad and early contestability discipline

## English source

That discipline serves the [Constitutional Tetrad](core_00.000_preamble.md#constitutional-tetrad), scaled to [material stake](core_00.000_preamble.md#material-stake), especially:
- **oversight**, **accountability**, and **timeliness** through early review before lock-in defeats:
  - scrutiny;
  - correction;
- **participation** where consolidation forecloses:
  - fair entry;
  - exit;
  - bargaining.

## zh draft

这项纪律服务于[宪法四柱](core_00.000_preamble.md#constitutional-tetrad)，并按[实质利害](core_00.000_preamble.md#material-stake)的大小调整，尤其体现为：
- 通过及早审查，在锁定妨碍以下事项之前实现**监督**、**问责**和**及时性**：
  - 审查；
  - 纠正；
- 当合并排除以下事项时保障**参与**：
  - 公平进入；
  - 退出；
  - 谈判。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One excerpt — where the Tetrad is carried

## English source

**Where Chapter One carries the Tetrad.** Every section names the legs it engages in its Trace. These sections carry them most directly:

| Tetrad leg | Principal Chapter One homes |
|---|---|
| **Participation** | [§16 Stewardship In Depth](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#16-stewardship-in-depth) and [§17 Consequential Stewardship](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#17-consequential-stewardship-the-steward-role) (consequential roles and voice); [§7 Freedom](#7-freedom-bounded-agency) (meaningful agency) |
| **Oversight** | [§16](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#16-stewardship-in-depth) and [§17](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#17-consequential-stewardship-the-steward-role) (distributed understanding, records), with [§16.4 Document at Action](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#164-document-at-action) (records made by the act, so inspection has something to inspect); [§18 Governance](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#18-governance-under-stewardship-discipline) (how authority is allocated); [§12](#12-systemic-evaluation-requirement) (auditing) |
| **Accountability** | [§16.4 Document at Action](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#164-document-at-action) (the act makes its own record, so answerability can be traced); [§18 Governance](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#18-governance-under-stewardship-discipline) (authority-scaled answerability); [§19 Incentive Alignment and System Capture](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#19-incentive-alignment-and-system-capture) (rewards must not hollow the legs) |
| **Timeliness** | [§16](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#16-stewardship-in-depth) and [§17](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#17-consequential-stewardship-the-steward-role) (catching and fixing trouble without avoidable delay) |

## zh draft

**四元结构在第一章中的主要落点。**每个部分的 Trace 都会指出其涉及的支柱。以下部分最直接地承载这些支柱：

| 四元结构支柱 | 第一章的主要对应部分 |
|---|---|
| **参与** | [§16 深度 Stewardship](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#16-stewardship-in-depth)和[§17 产生实际后果的 Stewardship](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#17-consequential-stewardship-the-steward-role)（产生实际后果的角色与发声）；[§7 自由](#7-freedom-bounded-agency)（有意义的能动性） |
| **监督** | [§16](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#16-stewardship-in-depth)和[§17](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#17-consequential-stewardship-the-steward-role)（分布式理解与记录），以及[§16.4 行为时记录](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#164-document-at-action)（行为产生自身记录，使检查有据可查）；[§18 治理](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#18-governance-under-stewardship-discipline)（权力分配）；[§12](#12-systemic-evaluation-requirement)（审计） |
| **问责** | [§16.4 行为时记录](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#164-document-at-action)（行为产生自身记录，因而可以追溯责任）；[§18 治理](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#18-governance-under-stewardship-discipline)（问责程度与权力相称）；[§19 激励对齐与系统俘获](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#19-incentive-alignment-and-system-capture)（奖励不得掏空支柱） |
| **及时性** | [§16](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#16-stewardship-in-depth)和[§17](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#17-consequential-stewardship-the-steward-role)（及时发现并纠正问题，避免可避免的延误） |

# §6 Trust — Basis and Principle Constraints

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §6

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Sentients need systems they can actually rely on. [**Trust**](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#trust) is the constitutional principle that makes reliance legitimate: systems must earn it through honest behavior and demonstrated reliability over time, not induce it through deception or concealment. [**Trustworthiness**](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#trustworthiness) is the track record that earns it. Trustworthiness is a named constituent of [**Flourishing**](#flourishing), and the Trust it earns is essential to [**Continuity**](#continuity) — durable coordination requires systems sentients can count on.

Trust connects the principle constraints to everyday shared life:
- [**Truth**](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#truth-constitutional-constraint) forbids deception.
- [**Safety**](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#safety-constitutional-constraint) limits how far reliance may go when real risk is present.
- [**Materiality**](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#materiality) determines how much must be shown and explained — the higher the stakes for sentients who depend on a system, the more that system must disclose and justify.
- [**Trust Degradation and Misleading Reliance**](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#trust-degradation-and-misleading-reliance) names the failure mode — when systems create, preserve, or score reliance in constitutionally misleading ways.

## Draft translation (zh)

有感知能力的存在需要能够真正依赖的系统。[**信任**](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#trust)是使依赖具有正当性的宪法原则：系统必须以诚实行为和经时间验证的可靠性赢得信任，而不能通过欺骗或隐瞒诱使依赖。[**值得信赖性**](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#trustworthiness)是赢得信任的履历。值得信赖性是[**繁荣**](#flourishing)的明确组成部分，而由此赢得的信任对[**连续性**](#continuity)至关重要——持久协调需要有感知能力的存在能够依靠的系统。

信任把原则约束与日常共同生活联系起来：
- [**真理**](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#truth-constitutional-constraint)禁止欺骗。
- 存在真实风险时，[**安全**](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#safety-constitutional-constraint)限制依赖的程度。
- [**重大性**](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#materiality)决定必须展示和解释多少内容——依赖系统的有感知能力的存在面临的利害越大，系统就必须披露并说明越多。
- [**信任退化与误导性依赖**](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#trust-degradation-and-misleading-reliance)指明一种失效模式：系统以违背宪法原则的误导方式建立、维持或评估依赖。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# §6 Trust — Failure of Trust and Certification Standard

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §6

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Trust fails when reliance is built or kept through suppression, deception, hidden risk-shifting, or similar tactics — including anything that materially undermines sentients' ability to detect and challenge systemic risk. Trust also fails when reliance is endorsed or continued while documented problems materially threaten a system's alignment with this Constitution or cause misalignment with it. The [**System Alignment Certification**](core_09.000_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#chapter-nine-part-a-system-alignment-certification--evaluation) process under [Chapter Nine](core_09.000_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#chapter-nine-system-alignment-certification) is where systems demonstrate that their trust claims hold up: certification must verify that a system's actual behavior matches its representations, on a contestable record — not merely on operator assertion.

## Draft translation (zh)

如果通过压制、欺骗、暗中转嫁风险或类似手段建立或维持依赖，信任就会失效；凡是实质性削弱有感知能力的存在发现并质疑系统性风险之能力的做法，都包括在内。即使已记录的问题实质性威胁系统与本宪法的一致性或导致其不一致，仍认可或继续这种依赖，信任也会失效。[第九章](core_09.000_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#chapter-nine-system-alignment-certification)下的[**系统一致性认证**](core_09.000_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#chapter-nine-part-a-system-alignment-certification--evaluation)程序要求系统证明其信任主张经得起检验：认证必须依据可受质疑的记录，而非仅凭运营方陈述，核实系统的实际行为是否符合其所呈现的内容。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN zh__core_01.000_values_principles__trust_plain_language_summary.md bytes=780 -->
# Chapter One §6 — Trust Plain-Language Summary

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §6 opening

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: shared systems ask sentients to depend on them — for safety, information, access, and coordination. Trust under this Constitution means that dependence must be earned by how systems actually behave, not manufactured through spin, secrecy, or hidden risk transfer.*

## Draft translation (zh)

*简言之，共享系统要求有感知能力的存在依赖它们来获得安全、信息、访问和协调。根据本宪法，这种依赖必须通过系统的实际行为赢得，不能靠粉饰宣传、保密或暗中转移风险来制造。*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §5 — Truth Constraint

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §5 opening

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: systems may not deceive, distort, suppress, or structure their output to mislead — and high-impact decisions must rest on honest evidence, stated methods, acknowledged uncertainty, and genuine openness to contrary findings.*

Truth is non-negotiable: be honest in how you work and in what you tell others. It is a core part of [**Flourishing**](#flourishing). It is also needed for [**Continuity**](#continuity), because anything built to last depends on honest evidence and an accurate picture of what is really going on. The detailed definitions, evaluation criteria, and compliance tests live in [**Chapters Two through Five**](core_02.000_definition_structure.md), especially [Epistemic Integrity](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#epistemic-integrity), [Truth (Constitutional Constraint)](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#truth-constitutional-constraint), [Materiality](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#materiality), [Dependency](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#dependency), and [Foreseeability](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#foreseeability-and-reasonably-foreseeable).

Systems may not undermine the ability of sentients to understand what is happening, make informed decisions, or verify what they are being told. That includes lying, distorting, hiding information, reporting only favorable results while leaving out unfavorable ones, or presenting things in ways designed to mislead.

## Draft translation (zh)

*简言之：系统不得欺骗、歪曲、压制信息，也不得以误导为目的组织输出；高影响决策必须依据诚实证据、明确说明的方法、承认的不确定性，并真正愿意接受相反发现。*

真相不可妥协：工作方式和告知他人的内容都必须诚实。真相是[**Flourishing**](#flourishing)的核心部分，也为[**Continuity**](#continuity)所必需，因为任何旨在持久的事物都依赖诚实证据以及对实际情况的准确认识。详细定义、评估标准和合规测试见[**第二至第五章**](core_02.000_definition_structure.md)，尤其是[认知完整性](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#epistemic-integrity)、[真相（宪法约束）](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#truth-constitutional-constraint)、[实质性](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#materiality)、[依赖](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#dependency)和[可预见性](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#foreseeability-and-reasonably-foreseeable)。

系统不得削弱有感知能力的存在理解正在发生什么、作出知情决定或核验所获告知的能力。这包括撒谎、歪曲、隐瞒信息、只报告有利结果而遗漏不利结果，或以旨在误导的方式呈现信息。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One excerpt — Two Constitutional Aims

## English source

It advances the following under the [Two Constitutional Aims](core_00.000_preamble.md#two-constitutional-aims):
- **Continuity** aim (contestable, durable productive conditions);
- **Flourishing** aim (livelihood, agency, and innovation pathways while alternatives remain real).

## zh draft

这项纪律在[两项宪法目标](core_00.000_preamble.md#two-constitutional-aims)下推动实现：
- **连续性**目标（可争夺、持久的生产条件）；
- **繁荣**目标（只要替代方案仍真实存在，就保障生计、行动能力和创新路径）。

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
